"""
AttendSure - Academic Structure Serializers (Zero N+1 Queries)
File: backend/apps/api/serializers/academics.py
"""

from rest_framework import serializers
from apps.academics.models import (
    AcademicYear,
    Enrollment,
    GradeLevel,
    Room,
    Schedule,
    ScheduleDay,
    Section,
    Subject,
)
from apps.common.utils import format_deduped_section_label


class RoomSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = Room
        fields = ['id', 'name', 'building', 'capacity', 'room_type', 'is_active']


class GradeLevelSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = GradeLevel
        fields = ['id', 'code', 'name', 'stage', 'level_order']


class AcademicYearSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    class Meta:
        model = AcademicYear
        fields = ['id', 'code', 'start_date', 'first_friday_june', 'end_date', 'is_active']


class SubjectSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    name = serializers.CharField(source='title', required=False)

    class Meta:
        model = Subject
        fields = ['id', 'code', 'title', 'name', 'grade_level', 'tier', 'subject_type', 'units']


class SectionSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    grade_level_name = serializers.CharField(source='grade_level.name', read_only=True)
    grade_level_code = serializers.CharField(source='grade_level.code', read_only=True)
    adviser_name = serializers.CharField(source='adviser.full_name', read_only=True)
    academic_year_code = serializers.CharField(source='academic_year.code', read_only=True)
    room_name = serializers.CharField(source='room.name', read_only=True)
    display_label = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    student_count = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    grade_level_id = serializers.IntegerField(write_only=True, required=False)
    adviser_id = serializers.IntegerField(write_only=True, required=False, allow_null=True)
    room_id = serializers.IntegerField(write_only=True, required=False, allow_null=True)

    class Meta:
        model = Section
        fields = [
            'id', 'name', 'code', 'grade_level', 'grade_level_id', 'grade_level_name',
            'grade_level_code', 'academic_year', 'academic_year_code',
            'adviser', 'adviser_id', 'adviser_name',
            'room', 'room_id', 'room_name', 'room_number', 'capacity',
            'track_strand', 'display_label', 'student_count', 'is_active',
        ]
        extra_kwargs = {
            'academic_year': {'required': False, 'allow_null': True},
            'grade_level': {'required': False},
            'adviser': {'required': False, 'allow_null': True},
            'room': {'required': False, 'allow_null': True},
            'room_number': {'required': False, 'allow_blank': True},
            'capacity': {'required': False},
            'code': {'required': False, 'allow_blank': True},
        }

    def get_display_label(self, obj) -> str:
        grade = obj.grade_level.name if obj.grade_level else ''
        return format_deduped_section_label(grade, obj.name)

    def get_student_count(self, obj) -> int:
        return sum(1 for e in obj.enrollments.all() if e.status == 'ENROLLED')

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        data = data.copy() if hasattr(data, 'copy') else dict(data)
        if 'grade_level_id' in data and 'grade_level' not in data:
            data['grade_level'] = data['grade_level_id']
        if 'adviser_id' in data and 'adviser' not in data:
            data['adviser'] = data['adviser_id']
        if 'room_id' in data and 'room' not in data:
            data['room'] = data['room_id']
        if not data.get('academic_year'):
            ay = AcademicYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first() or AcademicYear.objects.first()
            if ay:
                data['academic_year'] = ay.id
        return super().to_internal_value(data)

    def create(self, validated_data):
        if not validated_data.get('academic_year'):
            validated_data['academic_year'] = (
                AcademicYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first()
                or AcademicYear.objects.first()
            )
        return super().create(validated_data)


class ScheduleSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    section_name = serializers.CharField(source='section.name', read_only=True)
    grade_level_name = serializers.CharField(source='section.grade_level.name', read_only=True)
    subject_title = serializers.CharField(source='subject.title', read_only=True)
    subject_code = serializers.CharField(source='subject.code', read_only=True)
    faculty_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    days_of_week = serializers.CharField(required=False, default='MON-FRI')
    schedule_code = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    section_id = serializers.IntegerField(write_only=True, required=False)
    subject_id = serializers.IntegerField(write_only=True, required=False)
    faculty_id = serializers.IntegerField(write_only=True, required=False, allow_null=True)
    room_id = serializers.IntegerField(write_only=True, required=False, allow_null=True)

    class Meta:
        model = Schedule
        fields = [
            'id', 'schedule_code', 'section', 'section_id', 'section_name', 'grade_level_name',
            'subject', 'subject_id', 'subject_title', 'subject_code',
            'faculty', 'faculty_id', 'faculty_name', 'room', 'room_id', 'room_number',
            'days_of_week', 'start_time', 'end_time', 'is_active',
        ]
        extra_kwargs = {
            'section': {'required': False},
            'subject': {'required': False},
            'faculty': {'required': False, 'allow_null': True},
            'room': {'required': False, 'allow_null': True},
        }

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        data = data.copy() if hasattr(data, 'copy') else dict(data)
        if 'section_id' in data and 'section' not in data:
            data['section'] = data['section_id']
        if 'subject_id' in data and 'subject' not in data:
            data['subject'] = data['subject_id']
        if 'faculty_id' in data and 'faculty' not in data:
            data['faculty'] = data['faculty_id']
        if 'room_id' in data and 'room' not in data:
            data['room'] = data['room_id']
        return super().to_internal_value(data)

    def get_schedule_code(self, obj) -> str:
        return obj.schedule_code or f"SCH-{obj.id:04d}"

    def get_faculty_name(self, obj) -> str:
        return obj.faculty.full_name if obj.faculty else "Unassigned"

    def _sync_schedule_days(self, schedule: Schedule, days_text: str):
        mapping = {
            'MON-FRI': [1, 2, 3, 4, 5],
            'MWF': [1, 3, 5],
            'TTH': [2, 4],
            'SAT': [6],
        }
        day_lookup = {'MON': 1, 'TUE': 2, 'WED': 3, 'THU': 4, 'FRI': 5, 'SAT': 6, 'SUN': 7}
        clean = str(days_text or 'MON-FRI').upper().strip()
        if clean in mapping:
            day_nums = mapping[clean]
        else:
            day_nums = [day_lookup[p.strip()] for p in clean.split(',') if p.strip() in day_lookup] or [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]

        ScheduleDay.objects.filter(schedule=schedule).exclude(day_of_week__in=day_nums).delete()
        existing = set(ScheduleDay.objects.filter(schedule=schedule).values_list('day_of_week', flat=True))
        ScheduleDay.objects.bulk_create([
            ScheduleDay(schedule=schedule, day_of_week=d) for d in day_nums if d not in existing
        ])

    def create(self, validated_data):
        days_text = validated_data.get('days_of_week', 'MON-FRI')
        schedule = super().create(validated_data)
        self._sync_schedule_days(schedule, days_text)
        return schedule

    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        days_text = validated_data.get('days_of_week')
        schedule = super().update(instance, validated_data)
        if days_text is not None:
            self._sync_schedule_days(schedule, days_text)
        return schedule


class EnrollmentSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    student_name = serializers.CharField(source='student.full_name', read_only=True)
    lrn = serializers.CharField(source='student.lrn', read_only=True)
    section_name = serializers.CharField(source='section.name', read_only=True)
    grade_level = serializers.CharField(source='section.grade_level.name', read_only=True)

    student_id = serializers.IntegerField(write_only=True, required=False)
    section_id = serializers.IntegerField(write_only=True, required=False)

    class Meta:
        model = Enrollment
        fields = [
            'id', 'student', 'student_id', 'student_name', 'lrn',
            'section', 'section_id', 'section_name',
            'grade_level', 'academic_year', 'enrollment_date', 'status', 'remarks',
        ]
        extra_kwargs = {
            'student': {'required': False},
            'section': {'required': False},
            'academic_year': {'required': False, 'allow_null': True},
            'status': {'required': False},
        }

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        data = data.copy() if hasattr(data, 'copy') else dict(data)
        if 'student_id' in data and 'student' not in data:
            data['student'] = data['student_id']
        if 'section_id' in data and 'section' not in data:
            data['section'] = data['section_id']
        if not data.get('academic_year'):
            ay = AcademicYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first() or AcademicYear.objects.first()
            if ay:
                data['academic_year'] = ay.id
        return super().to_internal_value(data)

    def create(self, validated_data):
        if not validated_data.get('academic_year'):
            validated_data['academic_year'] = (
                AcademicYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first()
                or AcademicYear.objects.first()
            )
        return super().create(validated_data)

"""
AttendSure - Image & School Emblem Serializer Fields
File: backend/apps/api/serializers/base.py
"""

import base64
import hashlib
import os
import uuid
from django.conf import settings
from django.core.files.base import ContentFile
from rest_framework import serializers
from apps.api.views.common import resolve_logo_field, safe_photo_url

MAX_IMAGE_SIZE_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024
ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS = {'png', 'jpg', 'jpeg', 'webp'}


class FlexibleImageField(serializers.Field):
    """Handles ImageField uploads for Student, FacultyProfile, and UserProfile."""

    def to_representation(self, value):
        request = self.context.get('request')
        return safe_photo_url(value, request)

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        if data in (None, '', 'null'):
            return None

        if hasattr(data, 'read'):
            if data.size > MAX_IMAGE_SIZE_BYTES:
                raise serializers.ValidationError("Image file size exceeds the 5 MB limit.")
            return data

        if isinstance(data, str):
            clean = data.strip()
            if not clean or clean.lower() in ('none', 'null', 'undefined'):
                return None

            if clean.startswith('data:image') and ';base64,' in clean:
                try:
                    header, img_str = clean.split(';base64,', 1)
                    raw_ext = header.split('/')[-1].split(';')[0].lower()
                    ext = 'jpg' if raw_ext in ('jpeg', 'pjpeg') else raw_ext

                    if ext not in ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS:
                        raise serializers.ValidationError(
                            f"Unsupported image format (.{ext}). Allowed: PNG, JPG, WEBP."
                        )

                    decoded_data = base64.b64decode(img_str, validate=True)
                    if len(decoded_data) > MAX_IMAGE_SIZE_BYTES:
                        raise serializers.ValidationError("Decoded image exceeds the 5 MB limit.")

                    file_name = f"upload_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}.{ext}"
                    return ContentFile(decoded_data, name=file_name)
                except serializers.ValidationError:
                    raise
                except Exception as exc:
                    raise serializers.ValidationError(f"Invalid Base64 image: {exc}")

            if clean.startswith(('http://', 'https://', '/media/')):
                raise serializers.SkipField()

        raise serializers.SkipField()


class SchoolLogoField(serializers.Field):
    """
    Handles TextField emblem columns on SchoolProfile (school_logo, left_logo, right_logo).
    Writes uploaded files or Base64 images directly to media/school_logos/ on disk
    and stores the clean media URL path in PostgreSQL.
    """

    def to_representation(self, value):
        request = self.context.get('request')
        return resolve_logo_field(value, request)

    def _save_bytes_to_disk(self, raw_bytes: bytes, ext: str) -> str:
        if len(raw_bytes) > MAX_IMAGE_SIZE_BYTES:
            raise serializers.ValidationError("Logo image exceeds the 5 MB limit.")
        file_hash = hashlib.sha256(raw_bytes).hexdigest()[:16]
        filename = f"logo_{file_hash}.{ext}"
        target_dir = os.path.join(settings.MEDIA_ROOT, 'school_logos')
        os.makedirs(target_dir, exist_ok=True)
        target_path = os.path.join(target_dir, filename)
        with open(target_path, 'wb') as f:
            f.write(raw_bytes)
        media_prefix = settings.MEDIA_URL if settings.MEDIA_URL.startswith('/') else f"/{settings.MEDIA_URL}"
        if not media_prefix.endswith('/'):
            media_prefix += '/'
        return f"{media_prefix}school_logos/{filename}"

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        if data in (None, '', 'null', 'None', 'undefined'):
            return None

        if hasattr(data, 'read'):
            raw_bytes = data.read()
            name = getattr(data, 'name', 'logo.png')
            ext = name.rsplit('.', 1)[-1].lower() if '.' in name else 'png'
            ext = 'jpg' if ext in ('jpeg', 'pjpeg') else ext
            if ext not in ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS:
                ext = 'png'
            return self._save_bytes_to_disk(raw_bytes, ext)

        if isinstance(data, str):
            clean = data.strip()
            if not clean or clean.lower() in ('none', 'null', 'undefined'):
                return None

            if clean.startswith('data:image') and ';base64,' in clean:
                try:
                    header, img_str = clean.split(';base64,', 1)
                    raw_ext = header.split('/')[-1].split(';')[0].lower()
                    ext = 'jpg' if raw_ext in ('jpeg', 'pjpeg') else raw_ext
                    if ext not in ALLOWED_IMAGE_EXTENSIONS:
                        raise serializers.ValidationError(
                            f"Unsupported logo format (.{ext}). Allowed: PNG, JPG, WEBP."
                        )
                    decoded_bytes = base64.b64decode(img_str, validate=True)
                    return self._save_bytes_to_disk(decoded_bytes, ext)
                except serializers.ValidationError:
                    raise
                except Exception as exc:
                    raise serializers.ValidationError(f"Invalid Base64 logo image: {exc}")

            if clean.startswith(('http://', 'https://', '/media/')):
                raise serializers.SkipField()

        raise serializers.SkipField()

"""

import uuid
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import serializers
from apps.academics.models import AppNotification, FacultyProfile, GatePass
from apps.api.views.common import resolve_faculty_for_user


class GatePassSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    faculty_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    student_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    valid_until = serializers.DateTimeField(source='valid_to', read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = GatePass
        fields = [
            'id', 'pass_number', 'faculty', 'faculty_name', 'student',
            'student_name', 'issued_by', 'pass_type', 'reason',
            'valid_from', 'valid_to', 'valid_until', 'status',
        ]
        extra_kwargs = {
            'pass_number': {'required': False, 'allow_blank': True},
            'issued_by': {'required': False, 'allow_null': True},
        }

    def get_faculty_name(self, obj):
        return obj.faculty.full_name if obj.faculty else ''

    def get_student_name(self, obj):
        return obj.student.full_name if obj.student else ''

    def create(self, validated_data):
        request = self.context.get('request')
        if not validated_data.get('pass_number'):
            validated_data['pass_number'] = f"EP-{timezone.localdate().strftime('%Y%m%d')}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()}"
        if not validated_data.get('issued_by') and request and request.user.is_authenticated:
            issuer = resolve_faculty_for_user(request.user) or FacultyProfile.objects.filter(is_active=True).first()
            if issuer:
                validated_data['issued_by'] = issuer
        return super().create(validated_data)

        extra_kwargs = {
            'recipient': {'required': False, 'allow_null': True},
            'student': {'required': False, 'allow_null': True},
        }

    def get_student_name(self, obj):
        return obj.student.full_name if getattr(obj, 'student', None) else ''

"""
AttendSure - Student Serializer (Printed QR ID Only, Zero N+1 Queries)
File: backend/apps/api/serializers/students.py
"""

import base64
import uuid
from django.core.files.base import ContentFile
from rest_framework import serializers
from apps.academics.models import AcademicYear, Enrollment, Student
from apps.api.services.qr_security import sign_student_qr_payload
from apps.api.views.common import safe_photo_url


class StudentSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    current_section = serializers.SerializerMethodField(read_only=True)
    section_id = serializers.IntegerField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    full_name = serializers.CharField(read_only=True)
    gender = serializers.CharField(source='sex', required=False)
    qr_payload = serializers.SerializerMethodField(read_only=True)
    photo_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField(read_only=True)

    class Meta:
        model = Student
        fields = [
            'id', 'lrn', 'first_name', 'middle_name', 'last_name', 'suffix',
            'full_name', 'sex', 'gender', 'birthdate', 'mother_tongue', 'ethnic_group',
            'religion', 'house_street_sitio', 'barangay', 'municipality_city',
            'province', 'father_name', 'mother_maiden_name', 'guardian_name',
            'guardian_relationship', 'parent_contact', 'photo', 'photo_url',
            'qr_token', 'qr_payload', 'is_active', 'current_section', 'section_id',
            'created_at', 'updated_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'qr_token', 'created_at', 'updated_at']
        extra_kwargs = {
            'sex': {'required': False},
        }

    def get_qr_payload(self, obj) -> str:
        return sign_student_qr_payload(obj.lrn, obj.qr_token)

    def get_photo_url(self, obj):
        return safe_photo_url(obj.photo, self.context.get('request'))

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        data = data.copy() if hasattr(data, 'copy') else dict(data)
        if 'gender' in data and 'sex' not in data:
            data['sex'] = data['gender']

        photo_val = data.get('photo')
        if photo_val and isinstance(photo_val, str) and photo_val.startswith('data:image'):
            try:
                format_part, img_str = photo_val.split(';base64,', 1)
                ext = format_part.split('/')[-1]
                data['photo'] = ContentFile(
                    base64.b64decode(img_str),
                    name=f"student_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}.{ext}",
                )
            except Exception:
                data.pop('photo', None)
        elif photo_val in ('', None) or (isinstance(photo_val, str) and photo_val.startswith(('http://', 'https://', '/media/'))):
            data.pop('photo', None)

        return super().to_internal_value(data)

    def _active_enrollment(self, obj):
        enrollments = list(obj.enrollments.all())
        if not enrollments:
            return None
        for enr in enrollments:
            if getattr(enr, 'academic_year', None) and enr.academic_year.is_active and enr.section:
                return enr
        return enrollments[0]

    def get_current_section(self, obj) -> str:
        enr = self._active_enrollment(obj)
        return enr.section.name if (enr and getattr(enr, 'section', None)) else 'Unassigned'

    def to_representation(self, instance):
        ret = super().to_representation(instance)
        enr = self._active_enrollment(instance)
        ret['section_id'] = enr.section_id if enr else None
        return ret

    def create(self, validated_data):
        section_id = validated_data.pop('section_id', None)
        if not validated_data.get('sex'):
            validated_data['sex'] = 'Male'

        request = self.context.get('request')
        if request and request.user and request.user.is_authenticated:
            validated_data['created_by'] = request.user
            validated_data['updated_by'] = request.user

        student = Student.objects.create(**validated_data)

        if section_id:
            ay = AcademicYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first() or AcademicYear.objects.first()
            if ay:
                Enrollment.objects.create(
                    student=student,
                    section_id=section_id,
                    academic_year=ay,
                    status='ENROLLED',
                )
        return student

    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        section_id = validated_data.pop('section_id', None)
        request = self.context.get('request')
        if request and request.user and request.user.is_authenticated:
            validated_data['updated_by'] = request.user

        for attr, value in validated_data.items():
            setattr(instance, attr, value)
        instance.save()

        if section_id:
            ay = AcademicYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first() or AcademicYear.objects.first()
            if ay:
                Enrollment.objects.update_or_create(
                    student=instance,
                    academic_year=ay,
                    defaults={'section_id': section_id, 'status': 'ENROLLED'},
                )
        return instance

"""
AttendSure - School Profile & User Management Serializer (QR ID & Mobile App Only)
File: backend/apps/api/serializers/users.py
"""

import secrets
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.core.cache import cache
from django.core.files.base import ContentFile
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Q
from rest_framework import serializers
from apps.academics.models import (
    FacultyProfile,
    GeofenceSetting,
    SchoolProfile,
    Student,
    UserProfile,
)
from apps.academics.models.people import normalize_ph_phone
from apps.api.views.common import get_user_role
from .base import FlexibleImageField, SchoolLogoField, safe_photo_url

User = get_user_model()


class SchoolProfileSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    left_logo = SchoolLogoField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    right_logo = SchoolLogoField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    school_logo = SchoolLogoField(required=False, allow_null=True)

    principal_photo_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    principal_employee_id = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    principal_department = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    principal_email = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    principal_contact_number = serializers.SerializerMethodField()

    class Meta:
        model = SchoolProfile
        fields = '__all__'
        extra_kwargs = {
            'school_id': {'required': False},
            'school_name': {'required': False},
        }

    def to_internal_value(self, data):
        mutable = data.copy() if hasattr(data, 'copy') else dict(data)
        alias_map = {
            'school_address': 'address',
            'school_contact': 'contact_number',
            'school_email': 'email',
            'school_head': 'principal_name',
            'radius_meters': 'geofence_radius_meters',
            'geofence_radius': 'geofence_radius_meters',
            'emblem': 'school_logo',
            'main_logo': 'school_logo',
        }
        for src_key, dest_key in alias_map.items():
            if src_key in mutable and dest_key not in mutable:
                mutable[dest_key] = mutable[src_key]
        return super().to_internal_value(mutable)

    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        instance = super().update(instance, validated_data)
        if any(k in validated_data for k in ('latitude', 'longitude', 'geofence_radius_meters')):
            geofence = GeofenceSetting.objects.filter(is_active=True).first() or GeofenceSetting.objects.first()
            if geofence:
                if instance.latitude is not None:
                    geofence.latitude = instance.latitude
                if instance.longitude is not None:
                    geofence.longitude = instance.longitude
                if instance.geofence_radius_meters is not None:
                    geofence.radius_meters = int(instance.geofence_radius_meters)
                geofence.is_active = True
                geofence.save()
            elif instance.latitude is not None and instance.longitude is not None:
                GeofenceSetting.objects.create(
                    name='Campus Boundary',
                    latitude=instance.latitude,
                    longitude=instance.longitude,
                    radius_meters=int(instance.geofence_radius_meters or 150),
                    is_active=True,
                )
        return instance

    def get_principal_photo_url(self, obj):
        request = self.context.get('request')
        faculty = obj.principal_faculty
        if faculty:
            if faculty.photo:
                return safe_photo_url(faculty.photo, request)
            if hasattr(faculty, 'account') and faculty.account and faculty.account.avatar:
                return safe_photo_url(faculty.account.avatar, request)
            user = User.objects.filter(
                Q(username__iexact=faculty.employee_id) | Q(email__iexact=faculty.email)
            ).first()
            if user and hasattr(user, 'profile') and user.profile.avatar:
                return safe_photo_url(user.profile.avatar, request)
        return None

    def get_principal_employee_id(self, obj):
        return obj.principal_faculty.employee_id if obj.principal_faculty else ''

    def get_principal_department(self, obj):
        return obj.principal_faculty.department if obj.principal_faculty else 'Administration'

    def get_principal_email(self, obj):
        if obj.principal_faculty:
            return obj.principal_faculty.email or (
                obj.principal_faculty.account.user.email
                if hasattr(obj.principal_faculty, 'account') and obj.principal_faculty.account
                else ''
            )
        return ''

    def get_principal_contact_number(self, obj):
        return obj.principal_faculty.contact_number if obj.principal_faculty else ''


class UserManagementSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    role = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)
    current_role = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, read_only=True)
    is_teaching = serializers.BooleanField(required=False, default=True)
    can_teach = serializers.BooleanField(required=False, read_only=True)
    is_profile_completed = serializers.BooleanField(required=False, read_only=True)
    faculty_id = serializers.IntegerField(required=False, read_only=True, allow_null=True)

    employee_id = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)
    middle_name = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)
    suffix = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)
    full_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    contact_number = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)
    phone_number = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)
    department = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)
    position = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True)

    photo = FlexibleImageField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    photo_url = serializers.SerializerMethodField()
    password = serializers.CharField(write_only=True, required=False, allow_blank=True)

    class Meta:
        model = User
        fields = [
            'id', 'username', 'employee_id', 'first_name', 'middle_name', 'last_name',
            'suffix', 'full_name', 'email', 'contact_number', 'phone_number',
            'department', 'position', 'role', 'current_role', 'is_teaching',
            'can_teach', 'is_profile_completed', 'faculty_id', 'photo',
            'photo_url', 'is_active', 'is_staff', 'is_superuser', 'password',
        ]

    def _get_profile(self, obj):
        return getattr(obj, 'profile', None)

    def get_full_name(self, obj):
        prof = self._get_profile(obj)
        if prof and prof.faculty and prof.faculty.full_name:
            return prof.faculty.full_name
        return ' '.join(filter(None, [obj.first_name, obj.last_name])).strip() or obj.username

    def get_photo_url(self, obj):
        request = self.context.get('request')
        prof = self._get_profile(obj)
        if prof and prof.faculty and prof.faculty.photo:
            return safe_photo_url(prof.faculty.photo, request)
        if prof and prof.avatar:
            return safe_photo_url(prof.avatar, request)
        return None

    def validate_employee_id(self, value):
        if not value or not str(value).strip():
            return ''
        clean_id = str(value).strip().upper()
        qs = FacultyProfile.objects.filter(employee_id__iexact=clean_id)
        if self.instance:
            prof = self._get_profile(self.instance)
            if prof and prof.faculty_id:
                qs = qs.exclude(id=prof.faculty_id)
        if qs.exists():
            raise serializers.ValidationError(f"Employee ID '{clean_id}' is already registered.")
        return clean_id

    def to_representation(self, instance):
        ret = super().to_representation(instance)
        prof = self._get_profile(instance)
        faculty = prof.faculty if prof else None

        base_role = str(prof.role).upper() if (prof and prof.role) else ('ADMIN' if instance.is_superuser else 'FACULTY')
        is_teach = False if base_role == 'PARENT' else (bool(prof.is_teaching) if prof else base_role == 'FACULTY')

        ret['role'] = base_role
        ret['current_role'] = 'FACULTY_ADMIN' if (base_role == 'ADMIN' and is_teach) else base_role
        ret['is_teaching'] = is_teach
        ret['can_teach'] = is_teach
        ret['is_profile_completed'] = bool(prof.is_profile_completed) if prof else bool(instance.first_name and instance.last_name)
        ret['faculty_id'] = prof.faculty_id if prof else None
        ret['employee_id'] = faculty.employee_id if faculty else instance.username
        ret['middle_name'] = faculty.middle_name if faculty else ''
        ret['suffix'] = faculty.suffix if faculty else ''
        phone = (prof.phone_number if prof and prof.phone_number else '') or (faculty.contact_number if faculty else '')
        ret['contact_number'] = phone
        ret['phone_number'] = phone
        ret['department'] = faculty.department if faculty else ('Parent / Guardian' if base_role == 'PARENT' else '')
        ret['position'] = faculty.position if faculty else base_role.replace('_', ' ').title()
        photo_url = self.get_photo_url(instance)
        ret['photo'] = photo_url
        ret['photo_url'] = photo_url
        return ret

    @transaction.atomic
    def create(self, validated_data):
        password = validated_data.pop('password', None)
        raw_role = validated_data.pop('role', None) or self.initial_data.get('role', 'FACULTY')
        is_teaching_param = validated_data.pop('is_teaching', None)
        photo = validated_data.pop('photo', None)

        employee_id = str(
            validated_data.pop('employee_id', None) or self.initial_data.get('employee_id') or validated_data.get('username') or ''
        ).strip().upper()
        middle_name = str(validated_data.pop('middle_name', None) or self.initial_data.get('middle_name') or '').strip()
        suffix = str(validated_data.pop('suffix', None) or self.initial_data.get('suffix') or '').strip()
        raw_contact = (
            validated_data.pop('contact_number', None)
            or validated_data.pop('phone_number', None)
            or self.initial_data.get('contact_number')
            or self.initial_data.get('phone_number')
            or ''
        )
        contact_number = normalize_ph_phone(raw_contact)
        department = str(validated_data.pop('department', None) or self.initial_data.get('department') or 'Junior High School').strip()
        position = str(validated_data.pop('position', None) or self.initial_data.get('position') or 'Faculty I').strip()

        clean_role = str(raw_role).upper()
        if clean_role == 'FACULTY_ADMIN':
            final_role, final_is_teaching = 'ADMIN', True
        elif clean_role == 'PARENT':
            final_role, final_is_teaching = 'PARENT', False
        elif clean_role == 'FACULTY':
            final_role = 'FACULTY'
            final_is_teaching = bool(is_teaching_param) if is_teaching_param is not None else True
        else:
            final_role = clean_role
            final_is_teaching = bool(is_teaching_param) if is_teaching_param is not None else False

        is_admin = final_role == 'ADMIN'
        validated_data['is_staff'] = is_admin
        validated_data['is_superuser'] = is_admin

        user = User(**validated_data)
        if password and str(password).strip():
            user.set_password(str(password).strip())
        else:
            user.set_unusable_password()
        user.save()

        photo_bytes = None
        photo_name = getattr(photo, 'name', 'avatar.jpg') if photo else 'avatar.jpg'
        if photo and hasattr(photo, 'read'):
            photo_bytes = photo.read()
            photo.seek(0)

        profile, _ = UserProfile.objects.get_or_create(user=user)
        profile.role = final_role
        profile.phone_number = contact_number
        profile.is_teaching = final_is_teaching
        profile.is_profile_completed = bool(user.first_name and user.last_name)
        if photo_bytes:
            profile.avatar = ContentFile(photo_bytes, name=photo_name)
        profile.save()

        if final_role == 'PARENT':
            if contact_number:
                Student.objects.filter(parent_contact=contact_number, parent_user__isnull=True).update(parent_user=user)
            return user

        clean_emp_id = employee_id or user.username.upper()[:50]
        faculty_photo = ContentFile(photo_bytes, name=photo_name) if photo_bytes else None
        faculty, _ = FacultyProfile.objects.update_or_create(
            employee_id=clean_emp_id,
            defaults={
                'first_name': user.first_name or user.username,
                'middle_name': middle_name,
                'last_name': user.last_name or 'Staff',
                'suffix': suffix,
                'email': user.email or '',
                'contact_number': contact_number,
                'department': department,
                'position': position,
                'can_teach': final_is_teaching,
                'is_active': user.is_active,
                'qr_token': secrets.token_urlsafe(24),
                **({'photo': faculty_photo} if faculty_photo else {}),
            },
        )
        profile.faculty = faculty
        profile.save(update_fields=['faculty'])
        return user

    @transaction.atomic
    def update(self, instance, validated_data):
        request = self.context.get('request')
        is_admin_req = bool(
            request and request.user and request.user.is_authenticated
            and (request.user.is_superuser or get_user_role(request.user) == 'ADMIN')
        )

        password = validated_data.pop('password', None)
        raw_role = validated_data.pop('role', None) or self.initial_data.get('role')
        is_teaching_param = validated_data.pop('is_teaching', None)
        photo = validated_data.pop('photo', None)

        if not is_admin_req:
            raw_role = None
            is_teaching_param = None
            validated_data.pop('is_staff', None)
            validated_data.pop('is_superuser', None)
            validated_data.pop('is_active', None)

        raw_contact = (
            validated_data.pop('contact_number', None)
            or validated_data.pop('phone_number', None)
            or self.initial_data.get('contact_number')
            or self.initial_data.get('phone_number')
        )
        contact_number = normalize_ph_phone(raw_contact) if raw_contact is not None else None

        if password and str(password).strip():
            instance.set_password(str(password).strip())

        for attr, value in validated_data.items():
            if hasattr(instance, attr):
                setattr(instance, attr, value)
        instance.save()

        profile, _ = UserProfile.objects.get_or_create(user=instance)
        if raw_role and is_admin_req:
            clean_role = str(raw_role).upper()
            profile.role = 'ADMIN' if clean_role == 'FACULTY_ADMIN' else clean_role
            instance.is_staff = profile.role == 'ADMIN'
            instance.is_superuser = profile.role == 'ADMIN'
            instance.save(update_fields=['is_staff', 'is_superuser'])
        if is_teaching_param is not None and is_admin_req:
            profile.is_teaching = False if profile.role == 'PARENT' else bool(is_teaching_param)
        if contact_number is not None:
            profile.phone_number = contact_number
        if photo and hasattr(photo, 'read'):
            profile.avatar = ContentFile(photo.read(), name=getattr(photo, 'name', 'avatar.jpg'))
        profile.is_profile_completed = bool(instance.first_name and instance.last_name)
        profile.save()

        if profile.role == 'PARENT' and profile.phone_number:
            Student.objects.filter(parent_contact=profile.phone_number, parent_user__isnull=True).update(parent_user=instance)
        elif profile.faculty:
            fac = profile.faculty
            fac.first_name = instance.first_name or fac.first_name
            fac.last_name = instance.last_name or fac.last_name
            fac.email = instance.email or fac.email
            if contact_number is not None:
                fac.contact_number = contact_number
            fac.can_teach = profile.is_teaching
            fac.is_active = instance.is_active
            fac.save()

        cache.clear()
        return instance

"""
AttendSure - Non-Blocking Mobile Push & In-App Notification Service
File: backend/apps/api/services/push_service.py
"""

import json
import logging
import threading
import urllib.request
from apps.academics.models import AppNotification, UserProfile

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

EXPO_PUSH_URL = "https://exp.host/--/api/v2/push/send"


def _dispatch_expo_payload(payload: dict, notification_id: int | None = None):
    """Sends a push payload over HTTPS in a background thread so scans never lag."""
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            EXPO_PUSH_URL,
            data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"),
            headers={
                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                "Accept": "application/json",
            },
            method="POST",
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=4.0) as response:
            response.read()
        if notification_id:
            AppNotification.objects.filter(id=notification_id).update(is_pushed=True)
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning("Push delivery skipped or delayed: %s", exc)


def send_push_to_user(
    user,
    title: str,
    message: str,
    category: str = "ATTENDANCE",
    student=None,
    extra_data: dict | None = None,
) -> AppNotification | None:
    """Saves an in-app alert and dispatches a mobile push notification."""
    if not user or not getattr(user, "is_active", False):
        return None

    notif = AppNotification.objects.create(
        recipient=user,
        student=student,
        title=title[:150],
        message=message,
        category=category,
    )

    profile = getattr(user, "profile", None) or UserProfile.objects.filter(user=user).first()
    push_token = getattr(profile, "push_token", None) if profile else None

    if push_token and str(push_token).startswith(("ExponentPushToken", "ExpoPushToken")):
        payload = {
            "to": push_token,
            "sound": "default",
            "priority": "high",
            "title": title[:150],
            "body": message[:250],
            "data": {
                "notification_id": notif.id,
                "category": category,
                "student_id": student.id if student else None,
                **(extra_data or {}),
            },
        }
        threading.Thread(
            target=_dispatch_expo_payload,
            args=(payload, notif.id),
            daemon=True,
        ).start()

    return notif


def notify_student_parent(
    student,
    title: str,
    message: str,
    category: str = "ATTENDANCE",
    extra_data: dict | None = None,
):
    """Links and notifies the parent mobile app account assigned to a student."""
    if not student:
        return None

    parent_user = student.parent_user
    if not parent_user and student.parent_contact:
        matched_profile = UserProfile.objects.filter(
            role="PARENT",
            phone_number=student.parent_contact,
            user__is_active=True,
        ).select_related("user").first()
        if matched_profile:
            parent_user = matched_profile.user
            student.parent_user = parent_user
            student.save(update_fields=["parent_user"])

    if not parent_user:
        return None

    return send_push_to_user(
        user=parent_user,
        title=title,
        message=message,
        category=category,
        student=student,
        extra_data=extra_data,
    )


def send_silent_wake_up_push(push_token: str, action: str = "START_TRACKING_IN") -> bool:
    """Sends a silent high-priority wake-up signal to a faculty mobile phone."""
    if not push_token or not str(push_token).startswith(("ExponentPushToken", "ExpoPushToken")):
        return False

    payload = {
        "to": push_token,
        "priority": "high",
        "contentAvailable": True,
        "data": {"action": action},
    }
    threading.Thread(
        target=_dispatch_expo_payload,
        args=(payload, None),
        daemon=True,
    ).start()
    return True

"""
AttendSure - Anti-Clone HMAC-SHA256 QR ID Security Service
File: backend/apps/api/services/qr_security.py
"""

import hashlib
import hmac
from django.conf import settings


def sign_student_qr_payload(lrn: str, qr_token: str) -> str:
    """Creates a tamper-proof signed QR payload for printed student ID cards."""
    clean_lrn = str(lrn or '').strip()
    clean_token = str(qr_token or '').strip()
    message = f"{clean_lrn}:{clean_token}".encode('utf-8')
    digest = hmac.new(
        settings.SECRET_KEY.encode('utf-8'),
        message,
        hashlib.sha256,
    ).hexdigest()[:12].upper()
    return f"ATTENDSURE:{clean_lrn}:{clean_token}:{digest}"


def parse_and_verify_qr_payload(raw_scan: str) -> tuple[str, bool]:
    """
    Extracts the clean token/LRN from a USB QR scanner input and verifies
    its HMAC signature when present.
    Returns (extracted_token, is_signature_valid).
    """
    clean = str(raw_scan or '').strip().replace('\r', '').replace('\n', '').replace('\0', '')[:128]
    if not clean:
        return '', False

    parts = clean.split(':')
    if len(parts) == 4 and parts[0].upper() == 'ATTENDSURE':
        _, lrn, token, provided_sig = parts
        expected_sig = sign_student_qr_payload(lrn, token).split(':')[-1]
        is_valid = hmac.compare_digest(provided_sig.upper(), expected_sig.upper())
        return token.strip(), is_valid

    if len(parts) == 3 and parts[0].upper() == 'ATTENDSURE':
        return parts[2].strip(), True

    return parts[-1].strip(), True

"""
AttendSure - System & User Activity Audit Log Engine
File: backend/apps/api/views/activity.py
"""

import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime

from django.utils import timezone
from django.db.models import Q
from django.db.models.fields.json import JSONField

from rest_framework.views import APIView
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework import status, permissions, viewsets
from rest_framework.throttling import UserRateThrottle

from apps.academics.models import AuditLog

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# --- CYBERSECURITY & COMPATIBILITY HOTFIX ---
# Prevent TypeError when database drivers return pre-parsed JSON lists or dictionaries
_original_from_db_value = getattr(JSONField, 'from_db_value', None)


def _safe_from_db_value(self, value, expression, connection):
    if value is None or isinstance(value, (dict, list)):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, (str, bytes, bytearray)):
        try:
            return json.loads(value, cls=self.decoder)
        except Exception:
            return value
    return value


JSONField.from_db_value = _safe_from_db_value
# --------------------------------------------


class UserActivityLogsAPIView(APIView):
    """
    Central Activity Logs for Settings Screen:
    - Queries real database AuditLog records with zero mock data.
    - Faster data fetching: uses select_related to prevent N+1 queries.
    - Cybersecurity: Admins see system-wide logs; regular users only see their own actions.
    """
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    throttle_classes = [UserRateThrottle]

    def get(self, request):
        user = request.user
        search_query = request.query_params.get('search', '').strip()
        action_filter = request.query_params.get('action', 'ALL').strip().upper()
        date_param = request.query_params.get('date', '').strip()

        # 1. Determine administrative authority
        is_admin = bool(user.is_superuser or user.is_staff)
        if hasattr(user, 'profile') and getattr(user.profile, 'role', None):
            if str(user.profile.role).upper() in ['ADMIN', 'PRINCIPAL', 'SUPERUSER']:
                is_admin = True

        # 2. Faster Data Fetching: single database trip via select_related
        logs = AuditLog.objects.select_related('performed_by').all().order_by('-created_at')

        # 3. Cybersecurity Data Isolation: non-admins strictly isolated to their own actions
        if not is_admin:
            logs = logs.filter(performed_by=user)

        # 4. Action Type Filter
        if action_filter and action_filter != 'ALL':
            logs = logs.filter(action__iexact=action_filter)

        # 5. Calendar Date Filter
        if date_param:
            try:
                target_date = datetime.strptime(date_param, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
                logs = logs.filter(created_at__date=target_date)
            except ValueError:
                pass

        # 6. Fast Search across user, action, table, client IP, and record ID
        if search_query:
            query_filter = (
                Q(action__icontains=search_query)
                | Q(table_name__icontains=search_query)
                | Q(client_ip__icontains=search_query)
                | Q(record_id__icontains=search_query)
                | Q(performed_by__username__icontains=search_query)
                | Q(performed_by__first_name__icontains=search_query)
                | Q(performed_by__last_name__icontains=search_query)
            )
            logs = logs.filter(query_filter)

        # 7. Safe Fetch with Multi-Layered Driver Protection
        try:
            logs_subset = list(logs[:250])
        except TypeError:
            # Fallback if driver bypasses the class patch
            try:
                logs_subset = list(logs.defer('changed_fields')[:250])
            except Exception:
                logs_subset = list(
                    logs.only(
                        'id', 'action', 'table_name', 'record_id', 'created_at', 'client_ip', 'performed_by'
                    )[:250]
                )
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"[UserActivityLogsAPIView] Query error: {e}", exc_info=True)
            logs_subset = []

        results = []
        for item in logs_subset:
            # Resolve user display name safely without throwing AttributeError
            if item.performed_by:
                full = getattr(item.performed_by, 'get_full_name', lambda: '')()
                user_label = full.strip() if full else item.performed_by.username
            else:
                user_label = "System / Terminal"

            # Format details safely from strings, lists, or dictionaries
            details_parts = []
            if item.table_name:
                details_parts.append(f"Target: {item.table_name}")
            if item.record_id:
                details_parts.append(f"#{item.record_id}")
            if hasattr(item, 'changed_fields') and item.changed_fields:
                if isinstance(item.changed_fields, list):
                    clean_fields = [str(f) for f in item.changed_fields]
                    details_parts.append(f"Fields: {', '.join(clean_fields)}")
                elif isinstance(item.changed_fields, dict):
                    details_parts.append(f"Fields: {', '.join(item.changed_fields.keys())}")
                else:
                    details_parts.append(f"Fields: {str(item.changed_fields)[:80]}")

            detail_text = " • ".join(details_parts) if details_parts else "Audit record logged"

            # Convert to local time in standard 12-hour AM/PM format
            dt = item.created_at
            local_dt = timezone.localtime(dt) if timezone.is_aware(dt) else dt

            results.append({
                'id': item.id,
                'timestamp': local_dt.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %I:%M:%S %p'),
                'time_only': local_dt.strftime('%I:%M:%S %p'),
                'date_only': local_dt.strftime('%Y-%m-%d'),
                'user': user_label,
                'action': (item.action or 'AUDIT_RECORD').upper(),
                'module': (item.table_name or 'ACADEMICS').upper(),
                'details': detail_text,
                'ip_address': item.client_ip or '127.0.0.1',
                'status': 'SUCCESS',
            })

        return Response({
            'success': True,
            'is_admin': is_admin,
            'total_activities': len(results),
            'total': len(results),
            'records': results,
            'results': results,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

ActivityLogAPIView = UserActivityLogsAPIView

    def list(self, request):

"""
AttendSure - Classroom Attendance & Enrollment Full CRUD Views

from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import permissions, status, viewsets
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.academics.models import (
    DailyAttendanceSummary,
    Enrollment,
    Schedule,
    Student,
    SubjectAttendanceLog,
)
from apps.api.serializers import EnrollmentSerializer
from .common import (
    IsAcademicManagerPermission,
    format_deduped_section_label,
    format_full_student_name,
    get_faculty_student_ids,
    get_student_active_section,
    is_leadership_role,
    resolve_faculty_for_user,

class EnrollmentViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """Full CRUD for student section enrollments."""
    queryset = Enrollment.objects.select_related(
        'student', 'section', 'section__grade_level', 'academic_year'
    ).order_by('-id')
    serializer_class = EnrollmentSerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsAcademicManagerPermission]


class ClassroomAttendanceCRUDAPIView(APIView):
    """
    Full CRUD for Classroom Subject Attendance Records:
    - GET: List attendance records filtered by date, section_id, or schedule_id.
    - POST: Manually create or record an attendance entry for a student.
    - PUT / PATCH: Update an existing attendance status (PRESENT, LATE, EXCUSED, ABSENT, CUTTING).
    - DELETE: Delete an erroneous attendance log entry.
    """
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def _can_manage_student(self, user, student: Student) -> bool:
        if user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(user):
            return True
        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if not faculty:
            return False
        return student.id in get_faculty_student_ids(faculty)

    def get(self, request):
        user = request.user
        date_str = request.query_params.get('date')
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section_id')
        schedule_id = request.query_params.get('schedule_id')

        target_date = timezone.localdate()
        if date_str:
            try:
                target_date = timezone.datetime.strptime(date_str, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
            except ValueError:
                pass

        qs = SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(attendance_date=target_date).select_related(
            'student', 'schedule', 'schedule__subject', 'schedule__section', 'schedule__section__grade_level'
        ).order_by('-scanned_at')

        if not (user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(user)):
            faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
            if not faculty:
                return Response({'records': [], 'total': 0}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
            qs = qs.filter(student_id__in=get_faculty_student_ids(faculty))

        if section_id and str(section_id).isdigit():
            qs = qs.filter(schedule__section_id=int(section_id))
        if schedule_id and str(schedule_id).isdigit():
            qs = qs.filter(schedule_id=int(schedule_id))

        records = []
        for log in qs[:300]:
            sec = log.schedule.section if log.schedule else None
            grade_name = sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else ''
            records.append({
                'id': log.id,
                'student_id': log.student_id,
                'student_name': format_full_student_name(log.student),
                'lrn': log.student.lrn,
                'schedule_id': log.schedule_id,
                'subject_code': log.schedule.subject.code if (log.schedule and log.schedule.subject) else '',
                'subject_title': log.schedule.subject.title if (log.schedule and log.schedule.subject) else '',
                'section': format_deduped_section_label(grade_name, sec.name if sec else ''),
                'attendance_date': str(log.attendance_date),
                'status': log.status,
                'scan_method': log.scan_method,
                'scanned_at': timezone.localtime(log.scanned_at).strftime('%I:%M:%S %p') if log.scanned_at else '',
            })

        return Response({'records': records, 'total': len(records)}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @transaction.atomic
    def post(self, request):
        student_id = request.data.get('student_id')
        schedule_id = request.data.get('schedule_id')
        new_status = str(request.data.get('status', 'PRESENT')).upper().strip()
        date_str = request.data.get('attendance_date')

        if new_status not in {'PRESENT', 'LATE', 'CUTTING', 'ABSENT', 'EXCUSED'}:
            return Response({'error': 'Invalid attendance status.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        student = Student.objects.filter(id=student_id, is_active=True).first() if str(student_id).isdigit() else None
        if not student:
            return Response({'error': 'Valid student_id is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        if not self._can_manage_student(request.user, student):
            return Response({'error': 'Access denied for this student.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        section = get_student_active_section(student)
        schedule = Schedule.objects.filter(id=schedule_id).first() if str(schedule_id).isdigit() else None
        if not schedule and section:
            schedule = Schedule.objects.filter(section=section, is_active=True).order_by('start_time').first()
        if not schedule:
            return Response({'error': 'No active schedule found for this student.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        target_date = timezone.localdate()
        if date_str:
            try:
                target_date = timezone.datetime.strptime(str(date_str), '%Y-%m-%d').date()
            except ValueError:
                pass

        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(request.user) or schedule.faculty
        now_dt = timezone.now()

        log, _ = SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.update_or_create(
            student=student,
            schedule=schedule,
            attendance_date=target_date,
            defaults={
                'faculty': faculty,
                'status': new_status,
                'scan_method': 'MANUAL',
                'scanned_at': now_dt,
            },
        )

        if section:
            DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.update_or_create(
                student=student,
                attendance_date=target_date,
                defaults={
                    'section': section,
                    'status': new_status,
                    'remarks': 'Updated from class list.',
                },
            )

        return Response({
            'success': True,
            'id': log.id,
            'student_id': student.id,
            'student_name': format_full_student_name(student),
            'status': log.status,
            'attendance_date': str(log.attendance_date),
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)

    @transaction.atomic
    def put(self, request):
        record_id = request.data.get('id') or request.query_params.get('id')
        new_status = str(request.data.get('status', '')).upper().strip()

        if not record_id or not str(record_id).isdigit():
            return self.post(request)

        if new_status not in {'PRESENT', 'LATE', 'CUTTING', 'ABSENT', 'EXCUSED'}:
            return Response({'error': 'Valid status is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        log = SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.select_related('student', 'schedule__section').filter(id=int(record_id)).first()
        if not log:
            return Response({'error': 'Attendance record not found.'}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        if not self._can_manage_student(request.user, log.student):
            return Response({'error': 'Access denied for this record.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        log.status = new_status
        log.save(update_fields=['status'])

        if log.schedule and log.schedule.section:
            DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.update_or_create(
                student=log.student,
                attendance_date=log.attendance_date,
                defaults={
                    'section': log.schedule.section,
                    'status': new_status,
                    'remarks': 'Status updated by faculty.',
                },
            )

        return Response({
            'success': True,
            'id': log.id,
            'status': log.status,
            'message': 'Attendance record updated.',
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    def patch(self, request):
        return self.put(request)

    @transaction.atomic
    def delete(self, request):
        record_id = request.data.get('id') or request.query_params.get('id')
        if not record_id or not str(record_id).isdigit():
            return Response({'error': 'Valid attendance record id is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        log = SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.select_related('student').filter(id=int(record_id)).first()
        if not log:
            return Response({'error': 'Attendance record not found.'}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        if not self._can_manage_student(request.user, log.student):
            return Response({'error': 'Access denied for this record.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        student = log.student
        att_date = log.attendance_date
        log.delete()

        remaining = SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(student=student, attendance_date=att_date).order_by('-scanned_at').first()
        if remaining and remaining.schedule and remaining.schedule.section:
            DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.update_or_create(
                student=student,
                attendance_date=att_date,
                defaults={'section': remaining.schedule.section, 'status': remaining.status},
            )
        else:
            DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.filter(student=student, attendance_date=att_date).delete()

        return Response({'success': True, 'message': 'Attendance record removed.'}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

"""
AttendSure - Authentication & Identity APIs (Full Profile CRUD)
File: backend/apps/api/views/auth.py

import base64
import uuid
from django.core.files.base import ContentFile
from django.contrib.auth import authenticate, get_user_model
from django.contrib.auth.password_validation import validate_password
from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError as DjangoValidationError
from django.core.validators import validate_email
from django.db import transaction
from rest_framework import status
from rest_framework.authtoken.models import Token
from rest_framework.permissions import AllowAny, IsAuthenticated
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.throttling import AnonRateThrottle
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.academics.models import FacultyProfile, Schedule, SchoolProfile, UserProfile
from apps.common.utils import get_user_role, is_regular_faculty, safe_photo_url
from .common import resolve_faculty_for_user, resolve_logo_field

User = get_user_model()


class AuthLoginRateThrottle(AnonRateThrottle):
    rate = '10/minute'


class ValidationRateThrottle(AnonRateThrottle):
    rate = '60/minute'


def _process_uploaded_photo(raw_photo):
    if not raw_photo:
        return None

    if hasattr(raw_photo, 'read'):
        try:
            raw_photo.seek(0)
            photo_bytes = raw_photo.read()
            name = getattr(raw_photo, 'name', 'avatar.jpg')
            return ContentFile(photo_bytes, name=name)
        except Exception:
            return None

    if isinstance(raw_photo, str):
        clean = raw_photo.strip()
        if clean.startswith('data:image') and ';base64,' in clean:
            try:
                header, img_str = clean.split(';base64,', 1)
                raw_ext = header.split('/')[-1].split(';')[0].lower()
                ext = 'jpg' if raw_ext in ('jpeg', 'pjpeg') else raw_ext
                if ext not in {'png', 'jpg', 'jpeg', 'webp'}:
                    ext = 'jpg'

                decoded_bytes = base64.b64decode(img_str, validate=True)
                if len(decoded_bytes) <= 5 * 1024 * 1024:
                    filename = f"avatar_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}.{ext}"
                    return ContentFile(decoded_bytes, name=filename)
            except Exception:
                return None

    return None


def build_authenticated_user_payload(user, request=None):
    faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
    school = SchoolProfile.objects.first()
    profile = getattr(user, 'profile', None)
    assigned_role = get_user_role(user)

    emp_id = getattr(faculty, 'employee_id', user.username) if faculty else user.username
    first_name = (getattr(faculty, 'first_name', '') or user.first_name or '').strip()
    middle_name = (getattr(faculty, 'middle_name', '') or '').strip()
    last_name = (getattr(faculty, 'last_name', '') or user.last_name or '').strip()
    full_name = (
        getattr(faculty, 'full_name', '')
        if faculty
        else " ".join(filter(None, [first_name, middle_name, last_name])).strip() or user.username
    )

    photo_url = None
    if faculty and faculty.photo:
        photo_url = safe_photo_url(faculty.photo, request)
    elif profile and profile.avatar:
        photo_url = safe_photo_url(profile.avatar, request)

    teaching_class_count = Schedule.objects.filter(faculty=faculty).count() if faculty else 0
    can_teach = bool(getattr(faculty, 'can_teach', True)) and bool(getattr(profile, 'is_teaching', True))

    return {
        'id': user.id,
        'username': user.username,
        'first_name': first_name,
        'middle_name': middle_name,
        'last_name': last_name,
        'full_name': full_name,
        'faculty_name': full_name,
        'faculty_id': emp_id,
        'faculty_pk': getattr(faculty, 'id', None),
        'email': getattr(faculty, 'email', '') or user.email or '',
        'contact_number': getattr(faculty, 'contact_number', '') if faculty else '',
        'position': getattr(faculty, 'position', '') if faculty else '',
        'department': getattr(faculty, 'department', '') if faculty else '',
        'photo': photo_url,
        'photo_url': photo_url,
        'school_name': getattr(school, 'school_name', '') if school else '',
        'school_id': getattr(school, 'school_id', '') if school else '',
        'school_logo': resolve_logo_field(getattr(school, 'school_logo', None), request) if school else None,
        'left_logo': resolve_logo_field(getattr(school, 'left_logo', None), request) if school else None,
        'right_logo': resolve_logo_field(getattr(school, 'right_logo', None), request) if school else None,
        'role': assigned_role,
        'system_role': assigned_role,
        'is_profile_completed': bool(profile and getattr(profile, 'is_profile_completed', True)),
        'can_teach': can_teach,
        'is_teaching': can_teach or (teaching_class_count > 0) or is_regular_faculty(assigned_role),
        'assigned_class_count': teaching_class_count,
        'is_superuser': user.is_superuser,
    }


class DatabaseLoginView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [AllowAny]
    throttle_classes = [AuthLoginRateThrottle]

    def post(self, request):
        username = str(request.data.get('username', '')).strip()
        password = str(request.data.get('password', '')).strip()

        if not username or not password:
            return Response({'error': 'Username and password are required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        existing_user = User.objects.filter(username__iexact=username).first()
        profile = getattr(existing_user, 'profile', None) if existing_user else None

        if profile and hasattr(profile, 'is_locked') and profile.is_locked():
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Account locked due to excessive failed attempts. Try again in 15 minutes.'},
                status=status.HTTP_429_TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,
            )

        user = authenticate(request=request, username=username, password=password)
        if user is None:
            if profile and hasattr(profile, 'record_failed_attempt'):
                profile.record_failed_attempt()
            return Response({'error': 'Invalid credentials.'}, status=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED)

        if not user.is_active:
            return Response({'error': 'Account is inactive. Contact school admin.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        if profile and hasattr(profile, 'reset_failed_attempts'):
            profile.reset_failed_attempts()

        token, _ = Token.objects.get_or_create(user=user)
        return Response({'token': token.key, 'user': build_authenticated_user_payload(user, request)}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


LoginAPIView = DatabaseLoginView


class CurrentUserView(APIView):
    """
    Complete Profile CRUD for Authenticated Users:
    - GET: Read personal profile details.
    - POST: Complete/create initial profile information.
    - PUT / PATCH: Update name, email, phone, photo, or password.
    - DELETE: Delete or deactivate own account.
    """
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        return Response(build_authenticated_user_payload(request.user, request), status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    def post(self, request):
        return self.put(request)

    @transaction.atomic
    def put(self, request):
        user = request.user
        data = request.data
        files = request.FILES

        first_name = data.get('first_name') if 'first_name' in data else data.get('firstName')
        last_name = data.get('last_name') if 'last_name' in data else data.get('lastName')
        middle_name = data.get('middle_name') if 'middle_name' in data else data.get('middleName')
        suffix = data.get('suffix')
        contact_number = data.get('contact_number') or data.get('contactNumber') or data.get('phone')
        raw_photo = files.get('photo') or files.get('avatar') or data.get('photo') or data.get('avatar')

        user_updates = []
        if first_name is not None:
            user.first_name = str(first_name).strip()
            user_updates.append('first_name')

        if last_name is not None:
            user.last_name = str(last_name).strip()
            user_updates.append('last_name')

        if 'email' in data:
            clean_email = str(data.get('email', '')).strip()
            if clean_email:
                try:
                    validate_email(clean_email)
                except DjangoValidationError:
                    return Response({'error': 'Invalid email address format.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
                if User.objects.filter(email__iexact=clean_email).exclude(id=user.id).exists():
                    return Response({'error': 'Email is already used by another account.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
            user.email = clean_email
            user_updates.append('email')

        new_password = data.get('password')
        if new_password:
            clean_pw = str(new_password).strip()
            if len(clean_pw) < 8:
                return Response({'error': 'Password must be at least 8 characters long.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
            try:
                validate_password(clean_pw, user=user)
            except DjangoValidationError as exc:
                return Response({'error': list(exc.messages)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
            user.set_password(clean_pw)
            user_updates.append('password')

        if user_updates:
            user.save()

        photo_file = _process_uploaded_photo(raw_photo)

        profile, _ = UserProfile.objects.get_or_create(user=user)
        profile.is_profile_completed = bool(user.first_name and user.last_name)
        if photo_file:
            profile.avatar = photo_file
            profile.save(update_fields=['avatar', 'is_profile_completed'])
        else:
            profile.save(update_fields=['is_profile_completed'])

        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if not faculty:
            clean_emp_id = (user.username or 'EMP').strip().upper()[:50]
            faculty = FacultyProfile.objects.filter(employee_id__iexact=clean_emp_id).first()
            if not faculty:
                faculty = FacultyProfile.objects.create(
                    employee_id=clean_emp_id,
                    first_name=user.first_name or user.username,
                    middle_name=str(middle_name or '').strip(),
                    last_name=user.last_name or 'Staff',
                    suffix=str(suffix or '').strip(),
                    email=user.email or '',
                    contact_number=str(contact_number or '').strip(),
                    photo=photo_file,
                    is_active=True,
                )
            profile.faculty = faculty
            profile.save(update_fields=['faculty'])
        else:
            fac_updates = []
            if first_name is not None:
                faculty.first_name = user.first_name
                fac_updates.append('first_name')
            if last_name is not None:
                faculty.last_name = user.last_name
                fac_updates.append('last_name')
            if middle_name is not None:
                faculty.middle_name = str(middle_name).strip()
                fac_updates.append('middle_name')
            if suffix is not None:
                faculty.suffix = str(suffix).strip()
                fac_updates.append('suffix')
            if 'email' in data:
                faculty.email = user.email
                fac_updates.append('email')
            if contact_number is not None:
                faculty.contact_number = str(contact_number).strip()
                fac_updates.append('contact_number')
            if photo_file:
                faculty.photo = photo_file
                fac_updates.append('photo')

            if fac_updates:
                faculty.save(update_fields=fac_updates)

        return Response(build_authenticated_user_payload(user, request), status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    def patch(self, request):
        return self.put(request)

    @transaction.atomic
    def delete(self, request):
        """Allows users to delete or close their own profile."""
        user = request.user
        if user.is_superuser and User.objects.filter(is_superuser=True, is_active=True).count() <= 1:
            return Response({'error': 'Cannot delete the only system administrator account.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        Token.objects.filter(user=user).delete()
        user.delete()
        return Response({'success': True, 'message': 'Your account has been deleted.'}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


CurrentUserProfileView = CurrentUserView


class LiveFieldValidationAPIView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]
    throttle_classes = [ValidationRateThrottle]

    def _validate(self, field, val, exclude_id):
        val = str(val or '').strip()
        field = str(field or '').strip().lower()
        is_taken = False
        message = ''

        if field == 'username' and val:
            qs = User.objects.filter(username__iexact=val)
            if exclude_id and str(exclude_id).isdigit():
                qs = qs.exclude(id=int(exclude_id))
            is_taken = qs.exists()
            if is_taken:
                message = f"Username '{val}' is already taken."
        elif field == 'email' and val:
            try:
                validate_email(val)
                qs = User.objects.filter(email__iexact=val)
                if exclude_id and str(exclude_id).isdigit():
                    qs = qs.exclude(id=int(exclude_id))
                is_taken = qs.exists()
                if is_taken:
                    message = f"Email '{val}' is already in use."
            except DjangoValidationError:
                return {'valid': False, 'is_taken': False, 'message': 'Invalid email format.'}

        return {'valid': not is_taken, 'is_taken': is_taken, 'message': message}

    def get(self, request):
        return Response(self._validate(request.query_params.get('field'), request.query_params.get('value'), request.query_params.get('exclude_id')), status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    def post(self, request):
        data = request.data or request.query_params
        return Response(self._validate(data.get('field'), data.get('value'), data.get('exclude_id')), status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class RegisterPushTokenAPIView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request):
        token = str(request.data.get('push_token', '')).strip()
        if not token:
            return Response({'error': 'Push token required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        profile, _ = UserProfile.objects.get_or_create(user=request.user)
        profile.push_token = token
        profile.save(update_fields=['push_token'])

        return Response({'success': True, 'message': 'Push token registered successfully.'}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
