"""
AttendSure - Parent & Faculty Direct Chat API (Full CRUD)
File: backend/apps/api/views/chat.py
"""

from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.academics.models import (
    Enrollment,
    FacultyProfile,
    ParentFacultyMessage,
    Schedule,
    Student,
)
from apps.api.services.push_service import send_push_to_user
from .common import (
    format_deduped_section_label,
    format_full_student_name,
    get_faculty_student_ids,
    get_user_role,
    is_leadership_role,
    resolve_faculty_for_user,
    safe_photo_url,
)


def _resolve_faculty_for_student(student: Student) -> FacultyProfile | None:
    enr = (
        Enrollment.objects.filter(student=student, status='ENROLLED')
        .select_related('section', 'section__adviser')
        .order_by('-id')
        .first()
    )
    if enr and enr.section:
        if enr.section.adviser:
            return enr.section.adviser
        sched = (
            Schedule.objects.filter(section=enr.section, is_active=True, faculty__isnull=False)
            .select_related('faculty')
            .first()
        )
        if sched and sched.faculty:
            return sched.faculty
    return FacultyProfile.objects.filter(is_active=True).order_by('id').first()


class ChatThreadsAPIView(APIView):
    """Returns available conversation threads for the logged-in Parent or Faculty."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        user = request.user
        role = get_user_role(user)
        threads = []

        if role == 'PARENT':
            children = list(Student.objects.filter(parent_user=user, is_active=True).order_by('first_name'))
            child_ids = [c.id for c in children]
            enrollments = {
                e.student_id: e.section
                for e in Enrollment.objects.filter(student_id__in=child_ids, status='ENROLLED')
                .select_related('section', 'section__grade_level', 'section__adviser')
            }

            for child in children:
                sec = enrollments.get(child.id)
                faculty = (sec.adviser if sec and sec.adviser else None) or _resolve_faculty_for_student(child)
                if not faculty:
                    continue

                msgs = ParentFacultyMessage.objects.filter(student=child, faculty=faculty, parent=user)
                last_msg = msgs.order_by('-created_at').first()
                unread_count = msgs.filter(is_read=False).exclude(sender=user).count()

                grade_name = sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else ''
                threads.append({
                    'thread_key': f"{child.id}-{faculty.id}",
                    'student_id': child.id,
                    'student_name': format_full_student_name(child),
                    'student_lrn': child.lrn,
                    'section': format_deduped_section_label(grade_name, sec.name if sec else ''),
                    'faculty_id': faculty.id,
                    'faculty_name': faculty.full_name,
                    'parent_id': user.id,
                    'parent_name': user.get_full_name() or user.username,
                    'photo_url': safe_photo_url(faculty.photo, request),
                    'last_message': last_msg.body if last_msg else 'Tap to message class adviser',
                    'last_time': timezone.localtime(last_msg.created_at).strftime('%I:%M %p') if last_msg else '',
                    'unread_count': unread_count,
                })
        else:
            faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
            is_leader = bool(user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(user))

            if is_leader and not faculty:
                students_qs = Student.objects.filter(is_active=True, parent_user__isnull=False)
            elif faculty:
                allowed_ids = get_faculty_student_ids(faculty)
                students_qs = Student.objects.filter(id__in=allowed_ids, is_active=True, parent_user__isnull=False)
            else:
                students_qs = Student.objects.none()

            students = list(students_qs.select_related('parent_user').order_by('last_name', 'first_name')[:150])
            student_ids = [s.id for s in students]
            enrollments = {
                e.student_id: e.section
                for e in Enrollment.objects.filter(student_id__in=student_ids, status='ENROLLED')
                .select_related('section', 'section__grade_level')
            }

            for st in students:
                target_fac = faculty or _resolve_faculty_for_student(st)
                if not target_fac or not st.parent_user:
                    continue

                msgs = ParentFacultyMessage.objects.filter(student=st, faculty=target_fac, parent=st.parent_user)
                last_msg = msgs.order_by('-created_at').first()
                unread_count = msgs.filter(is_read=False).exclude(sender=user).count()

                sec = enrollments.get(st.id)
                grade_name = sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else ''
                parent_label = (
                    st.guardian_name
                    or st.mother_maiden_name
                    or st.father_name
                    or st.parent_user.get_full_name()
                    or st.parent_user.username
                )

                threads.append({
                    'thread_key': f"{st.id}-{target_fac.id}",
                    'student_id': st.id,
                    'student_name': format_full_student_name(st),
                    'student_lrn': st.lrn,
                    'section': format_deduped_section_label(grade_name, sec.name if sec else ''),
                    'faculty_id': target_fac.id,
                    'faculty_name': target_fac.full_name,
                    'parent_id': st.parent_user.id,
                    'parent_name': parent_label,
                    'photo_url': safe_photo_url(st.photo, request),
                    'last_message': last_msg.body if last_msg else 'No messages yet',
                    'last_time': timezone.localtime(last_msg.created_at).strftime('%I:%M %p') if last_msg else '',
                    'unread_count': unread_count,
                })

        threads.sort(key=lambda t: (t['unread_count'] > 0, bool(t['last_time'])), reverse=True)
        return Response({'threads': threads, 'total': len(threads)}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class ChatMessagesAPIView(APIView):
    """Full CRUD (Read, Send, Edit, Delete) for Parent-Faculty chat messages."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def _authorize_thread(self, user, student_id, faculty_id):
        student = Student.objects.select_related('parent_user').filter(id=student_id, is_active=True).first()
        if not student:
            return None, None, None, 'Student not found.'

        faculty = FacultyProfile.objects.filter(id=faculty_id, is_active=True).first() if str(faculty_id or '').isdigit() else None
        if not faculty:
            faculty = _resolve_faculty_for_student(student)
        if not faculty:
            return None, None, None, 'Assigned faculty not found.'

        role = get_user_role(user)
        if role == 'PARENT':
            if student.parent_user_id != user.id:
                return None, None, None, 'Access denied: This student is not linked to your parent account.'
            return student, faculty, user, None

        if not student.parent_user:
            return None, None, None, 'This student does not have a registered parent app account yet.'

        is_leader = bool(user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(user))
        own_fac = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if not is_leader:
            if not own_fac or student.id not in get_faculty_student_ids(own_fac):
                return None, None, None, 'Access denied: Student is not in your class list.'

        return student, faculty, student.parent_user, None

    def get(self, request):
        student_id = request.query_params.get('student_id')
        faculty_id = request.query_params.get('faculty_id')
        if not student_id or not str(student_id).isdigit():
            return Response({'error': 'Valid student_id is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        student, faculty, parent_user, err = self._authorize_thread(request.user, int(student_id), faculty_id)
        if err:
            return Response({'error': err}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        qs = ParentFacultyMessage.objects.filter(
            student=student,
            faculty=faculty,
            parent=parent_user,
        ).select_related('sender').order_by('created_at')

        qs.filter(is_read=False).exclude(sender=request.user).update(is_read=True)

        messages = [
            {
                'id': msg.id,
                'sender_id': msg.sender_id,
                'sender_name': msg.sender.get_full_name() or msg.sender.username,
                'is_mine': msg.sender_id == request.user.id,
                'body': msg.body,
                'is_read': msg.is_read,
                'created_at': timezone.localtime(msg.created_at).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %I:%M %p'),
                'time_only': timezone.localtime(msg.created_at).strftime('%I:%M %p'),
            }
            for msg in qs[:200]
        ]

        return Response({
            'student_id': student.id,
            'student_name': format_full_student_name(student),
            'faculty_id': faculty.id,
            'faculty_name': faculty.full_name,
            'messages': messages,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @transaction.atomic
    def post(self, request):
        student_id = request.data.get('student_id')
        faculty_id = request.data.get('faculty_id')
        body = str(request.data.get('body') or request.data.get('message') or '').strip()

        if not student_id or not str(student_id).isdigit():
            return Response({'error': 'Valid student_id is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
        if not body:
            return Response({'error': 'Message text cannot be empty.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
        if len(body) > 1000:
            return Response({'error': 'Message cannot exceed 1000 characters.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        student, faculty, parent_user, err = self._authorize_thread(request.user, int(student_id), faculty_id)
        if err:
            return Response({'error': err}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        msg = ParentFacultyMessage.objects.create(
            student=student,
            faculty=faculty,
            parent=parent_user,
            sender=request.user,
            body=body,
        )

        recipient_user = faculty.user if request.user.id == parent_user.id else parent_user
        sender_label = request.user.get_full_name() or request.user.username

        if recipient_user:
            transaction.on_commit(
                lambda: send_push_to_user(
                    user=recipient_user,
                    title=f"New Message ({student.first_name}): {sender_label}",
                    message=body[:180],
                    category='ANNOUNCEMENT',
                    student=student,
                    extra_data={'type': 'CHAT', 'student_id': student.id, 'faculty_id': faculty.id},
                )
            )

        return Response({
            'id': msg.id,
            'sender_id': msg.sender_id,
            'sender_name': sender_label,
            'is_mine': True,
            'body': msg.body,
            'is_read': False,
            'created_at': timezone.localtime(msg.created_at).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %I:%M %p'),
            'time_only': timezone.localtime(msg.created_at).strftime('%I:%M %p'),
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)

    @transaction.atomic
    def put(self, request):
        message_id = request.data.get('id') or request.data.get('message_id')
        new_body = str(request.data.get('body') or request.data.get('message') or '').strip()

        if not message_id or not str(message_id).isdigit():
            return Response({'error': 'Valid message id is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
        if not new_body or len(new_body) > 1000:
            return Response({'error': 'Updated message must be between 1 and 1000 characters.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        msg = ParentFacultyMessage.objects.filter(id=int(message_id)).first()
        if not msg:
            return Response({'error': 'Message not found.'}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)
        if msg.sender_id != request.user.id:
            return Response({'error': 'You can only edit your own messages.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        msg.body = new_body
        msg.save(update_fields=['body'])
        return Response({'success': True, 'id': msg.id, 'body': msg.body}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    def patch(self, request):
        return self.put(request)

    @transaction.atomic
    def delete(self, request):
        message_id = request.data.get('id') or request.query_params.get('id')
        if not message_id or not str(message_id).isdigit():
            return Response({'error': 'Valid message id is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        msg = ParentFacultyMessage.objects.filter(id=int(message_id)).first()
        if not msg:
            return Response({'error': 'Message not found.'}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)
        if msg.sender_id != request.user.id and not request.user.is_superuser:
            return Response({'error': 'You can only delete your own messages.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        msg.delete()
        return Response({'success': True, 'message': 'Message deleted.'}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

"""
AttendSure - Classroom Printed QR ID Scanner & Loafing Detection Engine
File: backend/apps/api/views/classroom.py

import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Q
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.academics.models import (
    DailyAttendanceSummary,
    Enrollment,
    Schedule,
    Section,
    Student,
    SubjectAttendanceLog,
)
from apps.api.serializers import ClassroomBatchScanSerializer
from apps.api.services.push_service import notify_student_parent
from .common import (
    format_deduped_section_label,
    format_full_student_name,
    get_user_role,
    is_leadership_role,
    resolve_faculty_for_user,
    safe_photo_url,
    verify_campus_gps,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def sanitize_qr_token(raw_token: str) -> str:
    """Cleans scanned QR ID strings and extracts LRN or token safely."""
    clean = str(raw_token or '').strip().replace('\r', '').replace('\n', '').replace('\0', '')[:64]
    if clean.startswith('{') and clean.endswith('}'):
        try:
            parsed = json.loads(clean)
            return str(
                parsed.get('lrn') or parsed.get('qr_token') or parsed.get('id') or clean
            ).strip()
        except Exception:
            pass
    return clean.split(':')[-1].strip() if ':' in clean else clean


def determine_scan_status(schedule, current_dt, grace_minutes: int = 15) -> str:
    """Returns PRESENT or LATE based on class start time plus grace minutes."""
    if not schedule or not getattr(schedule, 'start_time', None):
        return 'PRESENT'
    local_now = timezone.localtime(current_dt)
    class_start = timezone.make_aware(
        datetime.combine(local_now.date(), schedule.start_time),
        timezone.get_current_timezone(),
    )
    return 'LATE' if local_now > (class_start + timedelta(minutes=grace_minutes)) else 'PRESENT'


def serialize_class_list(students, request, subject_logs=None, daily_logs=None, schedule_id=None):
    """Formats a student class list consistently across all classroom endpoints."""
    subject_logs = subject_logs or {}
    daily_logs = daily_logs or {}
    items = []

    for st in students:
        att_status = (
            (subject_logs.get((schedule_id, st.id)) if schedule_id else subject_logs.get(st.id))
            or daily_logs.get(st.id)
            or 'UNSCANNED'
        )
        full_name = format_full_student_name(st)
        items.append({
            'id': st.id,
            'lrn': str(st.lrn).strip(),
            'full_name': full_name,
            'name': full_name,
            'gender': st.sex,
            'photo_url': safe_photo_url(st.photo, request),
            'attendance_status': att_status,
            'parent_contact': st.parent_contact or '',
            'qr_token': st.qr_token or '',
        })
    return items


class ClassroomStudentScanAPIView(APIView):
    """
    Scans a student's printed QR ID card from a faculty mobile phone or laptop,
    checks campus GPS boundaries, flags missed earlier periods, and notifies the parent app.
    """
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    @transaction.atomic
    def post(self, request):
        user = request.user
        role = get_user_role(user)
        now = timezone.now()
        today = timezone.localdate()

        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        is_admin = is_leadership_role(user) or user.is_superuser or role in ('ADMIN', 'SUPERADMIN', 'PRINCIPAL')

        if not faculty and not is_admin:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Only verified faculty, class advisers, and administrators can record attendance.'},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )

        lat = request.data.get('latitude')
        lon = request.data.get('longitude')
        if lat is not None and lon is not None and not is_admin:
            gps_check = verify_campus_gps(
                latitude=lat,
                longitude=lon,
                accuracy_meters=request.data.get('accuracy'),
                is_mock_location=bool(request.data.get('is_mock_location', False)),
            )
            if not gps_check['is_inside']:
                return Response({'error': gps_check['reason']}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        raw_code = str(
            request.data.get('code') or request.data.get('raw_identifier') or request.data.get('qr_token') or ''
        ).strip()
        cleaned_token = sanitize_qr_token(raw_code)
        if not cleaned_token:
            return Response({'error': 'No printed QR code received.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        # Printed QR ID lookup only (qr_token or LRN)
        student = Student.objects.filter(
            Q(qr_token=cleaned_token) | Q(lrn__iexact=cleaned_token),
            is_active=True,
        ).first()

        if not student:
            return Response(
                {'error': f'Unregistered student QR ID ({cleaned_token}).'},
                status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            )

        enrollment = (
            Enrollment.objects.filter(student=student, status='ENROLLED')
            .select_related('section', 'section__grade_level')
            .order_by('-id')
            .first()
        )
        student_section = enrollment.section if enrollment else None
        if not student_section:
            return Response(
                {'error': f'Student {student.full_name} is not enrolled in an active section.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        grade_name = student_section.grade_level.name if student_section.grade_level else ''
        section_display = format_deduped_section_label(grade_name, student_section.name)

        is_adviser = bool(faculty and student_section.adviser_id == faculty.id)
        requested_sched_id = request.data.get('schedule_id')

        active_schedule = None
        if faculty:
            sched_qs = Schedule.objects.filter(
                faculty=faculty,
                section=student_section,
                is_active=True,
            ).select_related('subject')
            if requested_sched_id and str(requested_sched_id).isdigit():
                active_schedule = sched_qs.filter(id=int(requested_sched_id)).first()
            if not active_schedule:
                active_schedule = sched_qs.first()

        is_subject_faculty = bool(active_schedule)
        if not (is_adviser or is_subject_faculty or is_admin):
            return Response(
                {'error': f'Access Denied: You are not assigned to {section_display}.'},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )

        role_label = 'Class Adviser' if is_adviser else ('Subject Faculty' if is_subject_faculty else 'Administrator')
        target_schedule = active_schedule or Schedule.objects.filter(
            section=student_section,
            is_active=True,
        ).order_by('start_time').first()

        if not target_schedule:
            return Response(
                {'error': f'No active subject schedule found for {section_display}.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        attended_schedule_ids = set(
            SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(
                student=student,
                attendance_date=today,
                status__in=['PRESENT', 'LATE'],
            ).values_list('schedule_id', flat=True)
        )
        missed_earlier_count = Schedule.objects.filter(
            section=student_section,
            is_active=True,
            start_time__lt=target_schedule.start_time,
        ).exclude(id__in=attended_schedule_ids).count()

        attendance_note = (
            f'LOAFING ALERT: Missed {missed_earlier_count} earlier subject period(s) today.'
            if missed_earlier_count > 0
            else 'Classroom QR attendance verified.'
        )
        photo_url = safe_photo_url(student.photo, request)

        existing_log = SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(
            student=student,
            schedule=target_schedule,
            attendance_date=today,
        ).first()

        if existing_log:
            timestamp_str = timezone.localtime(existing_log.scanned_at or now).strftime('%I:%M:%S %p')
            return Response({
                'success': True,
                'is_duplicate': True,
                'message': 'QR ID already scanned for this class period today.',
                'authorization_role': role_label,
                'student_id': student.id,
                'student_name': format_full_student_name(student),
                'lrn': student.lrn,
                'section': section_display,
                'status': existing_log.status,
                'scanned_at': timestamp_str,
                'attendance_note': attendance_note,
                'photo_url': photo_url,
            }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

        computed_status = determine_scan_status(target_schedule, now)
        SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.create(
            student=student,
            schedule=target_schedule,
            faculty=target_schedule.faculty or faculty,
            attendance_date=today,
            scanned_at=now,
            scan_method='QR',
            status=computed_status,
        )

        DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.update_or_create(
            student=student,
            attendance_date=today,
            defaults={
                'section': student_section,
                'status': computed_status,
                'remarks': attendance_note[:255],
            },
        )

        subject_label = target_schedule.subject.title if target_schedule.subject else 'Class'
        time_display = timezone.localtime(now).strftime('%I:%M %p')
        alert_category = 'CUTTING_ALERT' if missed_earlier_count > 0 else 'ATTENDANCE'
        alert_title = (
            f'Cutting Class Alert: {student.first_name}'
            if missed_earlier_count > 0
            else f'Class Attendance: {student.first_name} ({computed_status})'
        )
        alert_body = (
            f'{student.first_name} {student.last_name} scanned into {subject_label} at {time_display}, '
            f'but missed {missed_earlier_count} earlier period(s) today.'
            if missed_earlier_count > 0
            else f'{student.first_name} {student.last_name} was marked {computed_status} in {subject_label} at {time_display}.'
        )

        transaction.on_commit(
            lambda: notify_student_parent(
                student=student,
                title=alert_title,
                message=alert_body,
                category=alert_category,
                extra_data={'status': computed_status, 'subject': subject_label},
            )
        )

        return Response({
            'success': True,
            'is_duplicate': False,
            'message': f'Attendance recorded ({computed_status}) by {role_label}.',
            'authorization_role': role_label,
            'student_id': student.id,
            'student_name': format_full_student_name(student),
            'lrn': student.lrn,
            'section': section_display,
            'status': computed_status,
            'scanned_at': timezone.localtime(now).strftime('%I:%M:%S %p'),
            'attendance_note': attendance_note,
            'photo_url': photo_url,
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


ClassroomScanAPIView = ClassroomStudentScanAPIView


class StudentQRCardAPIView(APIView):
    """Returns real student QR ID card payloads for printing PVC or paper IDs."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section_id')
        qs = Enrollment.objects.filter(status='ENROLLED', student__is_active=True).select_related(
            'student', 'section', 'section__grade_level'
        )
        if section_id and str(section_id).isdigit():
            qs = qs.filter(section_id=int(section_id))

        cards = []
        for enr in qs.order_by('student__last_name', 'student__first_name')[:300]:
            st = enr.student
            sec = enr.section
            grade_name = sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else ''
            cards.append({
                'student_id': st.id,
                'lrn': st.lrn,
                'full_name': format_full_student_name(st),
                'grade_section': format_deduped_section_label(grade_name, sec.name if sec else ''),
                'qr_token': st.qr_token,
                'qr_payload': f'ATTENDSURE:{st.lrn}:{st.qr_token}',
                'photo_url': safe_photo_url(st.photo, request),
            })

        return Response({'cards': cards, 'total': len(cards)}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class ClassListAPIView(APIView):
    """Returns assigned subject classes and enrolled class lists in fast batch queries."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        user = request.user
        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        is_admin = is_leadership_role(user) or user.is_superuser
        view_mode = str(request.query_params.get('mode', '')).lower()
        today = timezone.localdate()

        if is_admin and view_mode == 'all':
            scheds = Schedule.objects.filter(is_active=True)
        elif faculty:
            scheds = Schedule.objects.filter(
                Q(faculty=faculty) | Q(section__adviser=faculty),
                is_active=True,
            ).distinct()
        elif is_admin:
            scheds = Schedule.objects.filter(is_active=True)
        else:
            scheds = Schedule.objects.none()

        scheds_list = list(
            scheds.select_related('section', 'section__grade_level', 'subject', 'faculty').order_by('start_time')
        )
        section_ids = {s.section_id for s in scheds_list if s.section_id}

        extra_advisory = []
        if faculty:
            extra_advisory = list(
                Section.objects.filter(adviser=faculty, is_active=True)
                .exclude(id__in=section_ids)
                .select_related('grade_level')
            )
            section_ids.update(sec.id for sec in extra_advisory)

        enrollments = (
            Enrollment.objects.filter(section_id__in=section_ids, status='ENROLLED', student__is_active=True)
            .select_related('student')
            .order_by('student__last_name', 'student__first_name')
        )

        students_by_section = {}
        all_student_ids = []
        for enr in enrollments:
            if enr.student:
                students_by_section.setdefault(enr.section_id, []).append(enr.student)
                all_student_ids.append(enr.student_id)

        subject_logs = {
            (row['schedule_id'], row['student_id']): row['status']
            for row in SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(
                attendance_date=today,
                student_id__in=all_student_ids,
            ).values('schedule_id', 'student_id', 'status')
        }
        daily_logs = dict(
            DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.filter(
                attendance_date=today,
                student_id__in=all_student_ids,
            ).values_list('student_id', 'status')
        )

        classes_data = []
        seen_sections = set()

        for s in scheds_list:
            sec = s.section
            if not sec:
                continue
            student_items = serialize_class_list(
                students_by_section.get(sec.id, []),
                request,
                subject_logs=subject_logs,
                daily_logs=daily_logs,
                schedule_id=s.id,
            )
            start_str = str(s.start_time)[:5] if s.start_time else ''
            end_str = str(s.end_time)[:5] if s.end_time else ''

            classes_data.append({
                'id': s.id,
                'schedule_id': s.id,
                'section_id': sec.id,
                'name': sec.name,
                'section_name': sec.name,
                'grade_level': sec.grade_level.name if sec.grade_level else '',
                'grade_level_code': sec.grade_level.code if sec.grade_level else '',
                'subject_code': s.subject.code if s.subject else '',
                'subject_name': s.subject.title if s.subject else '',
                'subject_title': s.subject.title if s.subject else '',
                'room': sec.room_number or s.room_number or '',
                'start_time': start_str,
                'end_time': end_str,
                'time_display': f'{start_str} - {end_str}'.strip(' -'),
                'total_students': len(student_items),
                'enrolled_count': len(student_items),
                'student_count': len(student_items),
                'students': student_items,
                'is_adviser': bool(faculty and sec.adviser_id == faculty.id),
            })
            seen_sections.add(sec.id)

        for sec in extra_advisory:
            if sec.id in seen_sections:
                continue
            student_items = serialize_class_list(
                students_by_section.get(sec.id, []),
                request,
                daily_logs=daily_logs,
            )
            classes_data.append({
                'id': sec.id,
                'schedule_id': sec.id,
                'section_id': sec.id,
                'name': sec.name,
                'section_name': sec.name,
                'grade_level': sec.grade_level.name if sec.grade_level else '',
                'grade_level_code': sec.grade_level.code if sec.grade_level else '',
                'subject_code': 'ADV',
                'subject_name': 'Advisory Homeroom',
                'subject_title': 'Advisory Homeroom',
                'room': sec.room_number or '',
                'start_time': '07:30',
                'end_time': '16:30',
                'time_display': 'All Day',
                'total_students': len(student_items),
                'enrolled_count': len(student_items),
                'student_count': len(student_items),
                'students': student_items,
                'is_adviser': True,
            })

        return Response(classes_data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


MyAssignedClassesAPIView = ClassListAPIView


class FacultyClassroomListAPIView(APIView):
    """Returns the enrolled class list for a specific section."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section_id') or request.query_params.get('section')
        if not section_id or not str(section_id).isdigit():
            return ClassListAPIView().get(request)

        today = timezone.localdate()
        students = list(
            Student.objects.filter(
                enrollments__section_id=int(section_id),
                enrollments__status='ENROLLED',
                is_active=True,
            )
            .distinct()
            .order_by('last_name', 'first_name')
        )

        subject_logs = dict(
            SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(
                student__in=students,
                attendance_date=today,
            ).values_list('student_id', 'status')
        )
        daily_logs = dict(
            DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.filter(
                student__in=students,
                attendance_date=today,
            ).values_list('student_id', 'status')
        )

        return Response(
            serialize_class_list(students, request, subject_logs=subject_logs, daily_logs=daily_logs),
            status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        )


StudentListAPIView = FacultyClassroomListAPIView
FacultyClassroomClassListAPIView = FacultyClassroomListAPIView


class ClassroomBatchScanAPIView(APIView):
    """Records multiple printed QR ID scans for a scheduled subject period."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    @transaction.atomic
    def post(self, request):
        serializer = ClassroomBatchScanSerializer(data=request.data)
        if not serializer.is_valid():
            return Response(serializer.errors, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        sched_id = serializer.validated_data['schedule_id']
        sched = Schedule.objects.select_related('section', 'subject', 'faculty').filter(id=sched_id).first()
        if not sched:
            return Response({'error': 'Active class schedule not found.'}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        today = timezone.localdate()
        sec = sched.section
        enrolled_ids = set(
            Enrollment.objects.filter(section=sec, status='ENROLLED').values_list('student_id', flat=True)
        )

        scans_data = serializer.validated_data['scans']
        clean_tokens = [sanitize_qr_token(item['qr_token']) for item in scans_data]

        matched_students = Student.objects.filter(
            Q(qr_token__in=clean_tokens) | Q(lrn__in=clean_tokens),
            is_active=True,
        )

        token_map = {}
        for st in matched_students:
            if st.qr_token:
                token_map[st.qr_token] = st
            if st.lrn:
                token_map[str(st.lrn).strip()] = st

        now_dt = timezone.now()
        now_time_str = timezone.localtime(now_dt).strftime('%I:%M:%S %p')
        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(request.user)
        processed_records = []

        for item in scans_data:
            token = sanitize_qr_token(item['qr_token'])
            st = token_map.get(token)
            if not st:
                continue

            is_enrolled = st.id in enrolled_ids
            final_status = str(item.get('status', 'PRESENT')).upper() if is_enrolled else 'UNVERIFIED'
            remarks_text = 'Classroom QR attendance verified.' if is_enrolled else 'Student belongs to a different section.'

            SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.update_or_create(
                student=st,
                schedule=sched,
                attendance_date=today,
                defaults={
                    'faculty': sched.faculty or faculty,
                    'status': final_status if final_status in ['PRESENT', 'LATE', 'CUTTING', 'ABSENT', 'EXCUSED'] else 'ABSENT',
                    'scanned_at': now_dt,
                    'scan_method': 'QR',
                },
            )

            if is_enrolled:
                DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.update_or_create(
                    student=st,
                    attendance_date=today,
                    defaults={
                        'section': sec,
                        'status': final_status if final_status in ['PRESENT', 'LATE', 'CUTTING', 'EXCUSED'] else 'ABSENT',
                        'remarks': remarks_text,
                    },
                )

            photo_url = safe_photo_url(st.photo, request)
            processed_records.append({
                'student_id': st.id,
                'name': format_full_student_name(st),
                'lrn': str(st.lrn),
                'photo_url': photo_url,
                'section': sec.name,
                'status': final_status,
                'is_anomaly': not is_enrolled,
                'remarks': remarks_text,
                'scan_time': now_time_str,
            })

        return Response({
            'success': True,
            'message': f'Classroom QR attendance recorded for {len(processed_records)} student(s).',
            'scanned_count': len(processed_records),
            'processed_records': processed_records,
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class AdviserSectionMatrixAPIView(APIView):
    """Cross-period attendance and cutting-class matrix for Class Advisers."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        user = request.user
        today = timezone.localdate()
        current_time = timezone.localtime(timezone.now()).time()
        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        is_admin = is_leadership_role(user) or user.is_superuser

        if is_admin:
            sections_qs = Section.objects.filter(is_active=True).select_related('grade_level').order_by('grade_level__level_order', 'name')
            advisory_ids = set()
        elif faculty:
            advisory_ids = set(Section.objects.filter(adviser=faculty, is_active=True).values_list('id', flat=True))
            teaching_ids = set(Schedule.objects.filter(faculty=faculty, is_active=True).values_list('section_id', flat=True))
            sections_qs = Section.objects.filter(id__in=(advisory_ids | teaching_ids), is_active=True).select_related('grade_level').order_by('grade_level__level_order', 'name')
        else:
            sections_qs = Section.objects.none()
            advisory_ids = set()

        available_sections = [
            {
                'id': s.id,
                'name': s.name,
                'grade_level': s.grade_level.name if s.grade_level else '',
                'is_adviser': s.id in advisory_ids,
            }
            for s in sections_qs
        ]

        raw_section_id = request.query_params.get('section_id')
        target_section = None
        if raw_section_id and str(raw_section_id).isdigit():
            target_section = sections_qs.filter(id=int(raw_section_id)).first()
        if not target_section and advisory_ids:
            target_section = sections_qs.filter(id=next(iter(advisory_ids))).first()
        if not target_section:
            target_section = sections_qs.first()

        if not target_section:
            return Response({
                'has_advisory_class': False,
                'message': 'No advisory or assigned teaching class found.',
                'available_sections': available_sections,
                'schedules': [],
                'matrix': [],
            }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

        schedules = list(
            Schedule.objects.filter(section=target_section, is_active=True)
            .select_related('subject', 'faculty')
            .order_by('start_time')
        )

        enrollments = (
            Enrollment.objects.filter(section=target_section, status='ENROLLED', student__is_active=True)
            .select_related('student')
            .order_by('student__last_name', 'student__first_name')
        )
        students = [e.student for e in enrollments if e.student]
        student_ids = [s.id for s in students]

        logs = SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(
            attendance_date=today,
            student_id__in=student_ids,
        ).values('student_id', 'schedule_id', 'status', 'scanned_at')

        daily_present_ids = set(
            DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.filter(
                attendance_date=today,
                student_id__in=student_ids,
                status__in=['PRESENT', 'LATE'],
            ).values_list('student_id', flat=True)
        )

        log_map = {}
        active_schedule_ids = set()
        first_seen_map = {}

        for entry in logs:
            st_id = entry['student_id']
            sc_id = entry['schedule_id']
            st_status = str(entry['status'] or '').upper()
            raw_dt = entry['scanned_at']

            active_schedule_ids.add(sc_id)
            time_label = timezone.localtime(raw_dt).strftime('%I:%M %p') if raw_dt else ''
            log_map[(st_id, sc_id)] = {'status': st_status, 'scan_time': time_label}

            if st_status in ['PRESENT', 'LATE']:
                daily_present_ids.add(st_id)
                if raw_dt and (st_id not in first_seen_map or raw_dt < first_seen_map[st_id]['dt']):
                    first_seen_map[st_id] = {'dt': raw_dt, 'time_label': time_label}

        matrix_rows = []
        for idx, st in enumerate(students, start=1):
            attended = 0
            cutting = 0
            subject_details = []
            in_campus_today = st.id in daily_present_ids

            for s in schedules:
                rec = log_map.get((st.id, s.id))
                if rec:
                    st_status = rec['status']
                    scan_t = rec['scan_time']
                    if st_status in ['PRESENT', 'LATE']:
                        attended += 1
                    elif st_status in ['ABSENT', 'CUTTING'] and in_campus_today:
                        st_status = 'CUTTING'
                        cutting += 1
                else:
                    period_started = bool(s.start_time and current_time >= s.start_time)
                    if in_campus_today and (s.id in active_schedule_ids or period_started):
                        st_status = 'CUTTING'
                        scan_t = ''
                        cutting += 1
                    else:
                        st_status = 'UNSCANNED'
                        scan_t = ''

                subject_details.append({
                    'schedule_id': s.id,
                    'subject_code': s.subject.code if s.subject else 'SUB',
                    'status': st_status,
                    'time': scan_t,
                })

            first_seen = first_seen_map.get(st.id)
            campus_status = f"First seen at {first_seen['time_label']}" if first_seen else (
                'Present in Class' if in_campus_today else 'No Class Scans Yet'
            )
            total_subjects = len(schedules)

            matrix_rows.append({
                'number': idx,
                'student_id': st.id,
                'student_name': format_full_student_name(st),
                'lrn': str(st.lrn),
                'photo_url': safe_photo_url(st.photo, request),
                'campus_status': campus_status,
                'presence_state': 'ACTIVE' if in_campus_today else 'NONE',
                'attended_count': attended,
                'cutting_count': cutting,
                'total_subjects': total_subjects,
                'compliance_rate': round((attended / total_subjects) * 100) if total_subjects > 0 else 0,
                'subjects': subject_details,
            })

        return Response({
            'has_advisory_class': True,
            'is_adviser': target_section.id in advisory_ids,
            'section_id': target_section.id,
            'section_name': target_section.name,
            'grade_level': target_section.grade_level.name if target_section.grade_level else '',
            'total_students': len(students),
            'available_sections': available_sections,
            'schedules': [
                {
                    'schedule_id': s.id,
                    'subject_code': s.subject.code if s.subject else 'SUB',
                    'subject_name': s.subject.title if s.subject else 'Subject',
                    'faculty_name': s.faculty.full_name if s.faculty else 'Unassigned',
                    'time_window': f'{str(s.start_time)[:5]} - {str(s.end_time)[:5]}' if s.start_time else '',
                }
                for s in schedules
            ],
            'matrix': matrix_rows,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


AdviserMatrixAPIView = AdviserSectionMatrixAPIView

"""
AttendSure - Offline-First USB QR Batch Sync & Signed QR ID Card Generator
File: backend/apps/api/views/classroom_sync.py
"""

from datetime import datetime
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Q
from django.utils import timezone
from django.utils.dateparse import parse_datetime
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.academics.models import (
    DailyAttendanceSummary,
    Enrollment,
    Schedule,
    Student,
    SubjectAttendanceLog,
)
from apps.api.services.push_service import notify_student_parent
from apps.api.services.qr_security import parse_and_verify_qr_payload, sign_student_qr_payload
from .classroom import determine_scan_status
from .common import (
    format_deduped_section_label,
    format_full_student_name,
    is_leadership_role,
    resolve_faculty_for_user,
    safe_photo_url,
)


class OfflineQRBatchSyncAPIView(APIView):
    """
    Syncs USB QR scans queued locally on a faculty PC or laptop while offline,
    preserving exact original scan timestamps and preventing duplicates.
    """
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    @transaction.atomic
    def post(self, request):
        user = request.user
        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        is_admin = bool(user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(user))

        if not faculty and not is_admin:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Only verified faculty and administrators can sync attendance.'},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )

        raw_items = request.data.get('scans') or []
        if not isinstance(raw_items, list) or not raw_items:
            return Response({'error': 'No offline QR scans provided.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        parsed_entries = []
        lookup_tokens = set()

        for entry in raw_items[:500]:
            raw_code = str(entry.get('qr_token') or entry.get('code') or '').strip()
            token, is_sig_valid = parse_and_verify_qr_payload(raw_code)
            if not token or not is_sig_valid:
                continue
            lookup_tokens.add(token)
            parsed_entries.append({
                'token': token,
                'schedule_id': entry.get('schedule_id'),
                'scanned_at': parse_datetime(str(entry.get('scanned_at', ''))) or timezone.now(),
            })

        students = Student.objects.filter(
            Q(qr_token__in=lookup_tokens) | Q(lrn__in=lookup_tokens),
            is_active=True,
        )
        student_map = {}
        for st in students:
            if st.qr_token:
                student_map[st.qr_token] = st
            if st.lrn:
                student_map[str(st.lrn).strip()] = st

        student_ids = [st.id for st in students]
        enrollments_map = {
            enr.student_id: enr.section
            for enr in Enrollment.objects.filter(student_id__in=student_ids, status='ENROLLED')
            .select_related('section', 'section__grade_level')
        }

        synced_count = 0
        duplicate_count = 0
        rejected_count = len(raw_items) - len(parsed_entries)

        for item in parsed_entries:
            st = student_map.get(item['token'])
            sec = enrollments_map.get(st.id) if st else None
            if not st or not sec:
                rejected_count += 1
                continue

            sched_id = item['schedule_id']
            target_schedule = None
            if sched_id and str(sched_id).isdigit():
                target_schedule = Schedule.objects.filter(id=int(sched_id), section=sec, is_active=True).select_related('subject').first()
            if not target_schedule and faculty:
                target_schedule = Schedule.objects.filter(faculty=faculty, section=sec, is_active=True).select_related('subject').first()
            if not target_schedule:
                target_schedule = Schedule.objects.filter(section=sec, is_active=True).select_related('subject').order_by('start_time').first()

            if not target_schedule:
                rejected_count += 1
                continue

            scan_dt = item['scanned_at']
            if timezone.is_naive(scan_dt):
                scan_dt = timezone.make_aware(scan_dt, timezone.get_current_timezone())
            scan_date = timezone.localtime(scan_dt).date()

            computed_status = determine_scan_status(target_schedule, scan_dt)
            _, created = SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.get_or_create(
                student=st,
                schedule=target_schedule,
                attendance_date=scan_date,
                defaults={
                    'faculty': target_schedule.faculty or faculty,
                    'scanned_at': scan_dt,
                    'scan_method': 'QR',
                    'status': computed_status,
                },
            )

            if not created:
                duplicate_count += 1
                continue

            DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.update_or_create(
                student=st,
                attendance_date=scan_date,
                defaults={
                    'section': sec,
                    'status': computed_status,
                    'remarks': 'Synced from offline USB QR station.',
                },
            )
            synced_count += 1

            subj_title = target_schedule.subject.title if target_schedule.subject else 'Class'
            time_label = timezone.localtime(scan_dt).strftime('%I:%M %p')
            notify_student_parent(
                student=st,
                title=f'Class Attendance: {st.first_name} ({computed_status})',
                message=f'{st.first_name} {st.last_name} scanned into {subj_title} at {time_label}.',
                category='ATTENDANCE',
            )

        return Response({
            'success': True,
            'synced_count': synced_count,
            'duplicate_count': duplicate_count,
            'rejected_count': rejected_count,
            'message': f"Successfully synced {synced_count} offline QR scan(s).",
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

class SignedStudentQRCardAPIView(APIView):
    """Generates anti-clone HMAC-signed QR ID payloads for printing student cards."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section_id')
        qs = Enrollment.objects.filter(status='ENROLLED', student__is_active=True).select_related(
            'student', 'section', 'section__grade_level'
        )
        if section_id and str(section_id).isdigit():
            qs = qs.filter(section_id=int(section_id))

        cards = []
        for enr in qs.order_by('student__last_name', 'student__first_name')[:500]:
            st = enr.student
            sec = enr.section
            grade_name = sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else ''
            cards.append({
                'student_id': st.id,
                'lrn': st.lrn,
                'full_name': format_full_student_name(st),
                'grade_section': format_deduped_section_label(grade_name, sec.name if sec else ''),
                'qr_token': st.qr_token,
                'qr_payload': sign_student_qr_payload(st.lrn, st.qr_token),
                'photo_url': safe_photo_url(st.photo, request),
            })

        return Response({'cards': cards, 'total': len(cards)}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

"""
AttendSure - Shared Database Resolvers, GPS Boundary Check & Security Helpers
File: backend/apps/api/views/common.py
"""

import base64
import hashlib
import logging
import math
import os
from django.conf import settings
from django.db.models import Q
from rest_framework import permissions

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def safe_photo_url(photo_field, request=None):
    """Returns a valid storage URL for student, faculty, or school logo photos."""
    if not photo_field:
        return None
    try:
        if isinstance(photo_field, str):
            val = photo_field.strip()
            if not val or val.lower() in ('none', 'null', 'undefined'):
                return None
            if val.startswith(('data:image/', 'http://', 'https://', '/media/')):
                return request.build_absolute_uri(val) if (request and val.startswith('/media/')) else val
            clean_path = val if val.startswith('/') else f'/{val}'
            return request.build_absolute_uri(clean_path) if request else clean_path

        if hasattr(photo_field, 'url'):
            try:
                url = photo_field.url
                if url:
                    return request.build_absolute_uri(url) if request else url
            except Exception:
                pass

        if hasattr(photo_field, 'name') and photo_field.name:
            val_name = str(photo_field.name).strip()
            if not val_name:
                return None
            if val_name.startswith(('data:image/', 'http://', 'https://', '/media/')):
                return val_name
            media_url = f"{settings.MEDIA_URL}{val_name}" if not val_name.startswith('/') else val_name
            return request.build_absolute_uri(media_url) if request else media_url
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning('Failed to resolve photo URL: %s', exc)
    return None


def resolve_logo_field(val, request=None):
    """Resolves school logos to a valid full URL or saves Base64 data to disk."""
    if not val:
        return None
    val_str = str(val).strip()
    if not val_str or val_str.lower() in ('none', 'null', 'undefined'):
        return None

    if val_str.startswith('upload_') and '/' not in val_str:
        candidate = os.path.join(settings.MEDIA_ROOT, val_str)
        if not os.path.exists(candidate):
            return None

    if val_str.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
        return val_str

    if val_str.startswith(('/media/', 'media/', 'school_logos/')):
        if val_str.startswith('school_logos/'):
            clean_rel = f'{settings.MEDIA_URL}{val_str}'
        else:
            clean_rel = val_str
        if not clean_rel.startswith('/'):
            clean_rel = f'/{clean_rel}'
        return request.build_absolute_uri(clean_rel) if request else clean_rel

    if val_str.startswith('data:image/') or val_str.startswith(('/9j/', 'iVBORw0KGgo')):
        try:
            if ';base64,' in val_str:
                format_part, img_str = val_str.split(';base64,', 1)
                ext = 'jpg' if ('jpeg' in format_part or 'pjpeg' in format_part) else format_part.split('/')[-1].split(';')[0].lower()
            else:
                img_str, ext = val_str, 'png'

            if ext not in {'png', 'jpg', 'jpeg', 'webp'}:
                ext = 'png'

            decoded_bytes = base64.b64decode(img_str, validate=True)
            if len(decoded_bytes) <= 5 * 1024 * 1024:
                file_hash = hashlib.sha256(decoded_bytes).hexdigest()[:16]
                filename = f'logo_{file_hash}.{ext}'
                target_dir = os.path.join(settings.MEDIA_ROOT, 'school_logos')
                os.makedirs(target_dir, exist_ok=True)
                target_path = os.path.join(target_dir, filename)

                if not os.path.exists(target_path):
                    with open(target_path, 'wb') as f:
                        f.write(decoded_bytes)

                media_url = f'{settings.MEDIA_URL}school_logos/{filename}'
                if not media_url.startswith('/'):
                    media_url = f'/{media_url}'
                return request.build_absolute_uri(media_url) if request else media_url
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error('Failed to decode logo to disk: %s', exc)
    return None


def format_full_student_name(student) -> str:
    """Formats student names directly from database columns."""
    if not student:
        return ''
    if hasattr(student, 'full_name') and isinstance(student.full_name, str) and student.full_name.strip():
        return student.full_name.strip()
    last = getattr(student, 'last_name', '') or ''
    first = getattr(student, 'first_name', '') or ''
    mid = f' {student.middle_name[:1]}.' if getattr(student, 'middle_name', None) else ''
    suf = f' {student.suffix}' if getattr(student, 'suffix', None) else ''
    return f'{last}, {first}{mid}{suf}'.strip(' ,')


def format_deduped_section_label(grade_name, section_name) -> str:
    g = str(grade_name or '').strip()
    s = str(section_name or '').strip()
    if not g:
        return s
    if not s or g.lower().endswith(s.lower()) or f'- {s.lower()}' in g.lower():
        return g
    return f'{g} - {s}'


def get_user_role(user) -> str:
    """Returns the role assigned in UserProfile."""
    if not user or not user.is_authenticated:
        return ''
    if getattr(user, 'is_superuser', False):
        return 'ADMIN'
    profile = getattr(user, 'profile', None)
    if profile and getattr(profile, 'role', None):
        return str(profile.role).upper()
    if getattr(user, 'is_staff', False):
        return 'ADMIN'
    return 'FACULTY'


def is_regular_faculty(role: str) -> bool:
    return str(role or '').upper() in {'FACULTY', 'EMPLOYEE'}


def is_leadership_role(user) -> bool:
    if not user or not user.is_authenticated:
        return False
    if getattr(user, 'is_superuser', False):
        return True
    return get_user_role(user) in {'ADMIN', 'SUPERADMIN', 'PRINCIPAL', 'DEPT_HEAD'}


def resolve_faculty_for_user(user):
    """Connects a Django User to their linked FacultyProfile row cleanly."""
    if not user or not user.is_authenticated:
        return None

    from apps.academics.models import FacultyProfile, UserProfile

    user_prof = getattr(user, 'profile', None) or UserProfile.objects.filter(user=user).select_related('faculty').first()
    if user_prof and user_prof.faculty_id and user_prof.faculty:
        return user_prof.faculty

    faculty = FacultyProfile.objects.filter(account__user=user, is_active=True).first()
    if faculty:
        return faculty

    if getattr(user, 'username', None):
        faculty = FacultyProfile.objects.filter(
            Q(employee_id__iexact=user.username) | Q(qr_token__iexact=user.username),
            is_active=True,
        ).first()
        if faculty:
            return faculty

    if getattr(user, 'email', None):
        faculty = FacultyProfile.objects.filter(email__iexact=user.email, is_active=True).first()
        if faculty:
            return faculty

    return None


def _get_active_campus_center():
    """Returns (latitude, longitude, radius_meters) from active campus settings."""
    from apps.academics.models import GeofenceSetting, SchoolProfile

    geofence = GeofenceSetting.objects.filter(is_active=True).first()
    if geofence and geofence.latitude is not None and geofence.longitude is not None:
        return float(geofence.latitude), float(geofence.longitude), int(geofence.radius_meters or 150)

    school = SchoolProfile.objects.first()
    if school and school.latitude is not None and school.longitude is not None:
        return float(school.latitude), float(school.longitude), int(school.geofence_radius_meters or 150)

    return None, None, 150


def calculate_distance_meters(lat1, lon1, lat2=None, lon2=None) -> float:
    """Calculates physical distance in meters using the Haversine formula."""
    try:
        u_lat = float(lat1)
        u_lon = float(lon1)
        if lat2 is None or lon2 is None:
            c_lat, c_lon, _ = _get_active_campus_center()
            if c_lat is None or c_lon is None:
                return 0.0
        else:
            c_lat = float(lat2)
            c_lon = float(lon2)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return float('inf')

    earth_radius = 6371000.0
    phi1 = math.radians(c_lat)
    phi2 = math.radians(u_lat)
    d_phi = math.radians(u_lat - c_lat)
    d_lambda = math.radians(u_lon - c_lon)

    a = (math.sin(d_phi / 2.0) ** 2) + math.cos(phi1) * math.cos(phi2) * (math.sin(d_lambda / 2.0) ** 2)
    return earth_radius * (2.0 * math.atan2(math.sqrt(a), math.sqrt(1.0 - a)))


def verify_campus_gps(latitude, longitude, accuracy_meters=None, is_mock_location=False) -> dict:
    """
    Verifies whether a faculty mobile phone is inside the campus boundary,
    including anti-spoofing and an indoor GPS drift buffer.
    """
    if is_mock_location:
        return {
            'is_valid': False,
            'is_inside': False,
            'distance_meters': None,
            'allowed_radius': 0,
            'reason': 'Fake or mock GPS location detected. Disable mock location apps to proceed.',
        }

    try:
        u_lat = float(latitude)
        u_lon = float(longitude)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return {
            'is_valid': False,
            'is_inside': False,
            'distance_meters': None,
            'allowed_radius': 0,
            'reason': 'Invalid or missing GPS coordinates.',
        }

    if not (-90.0 <= u_lat <= 90.0) or not (-180.0 <= u_lon <= 180.0) or (u_lat == 0.0 and u_lon == 0.0):
        return {
            'is_valid': False,
            'is_inside': False,
            'distance_meters': None,
            'allowed_radius': 0,
            'reason': 'GPS coordinates are outside valid earth bounds.',
        }

    c_lat, c_lon, radius = _get_active_campus_center()
    if c_lat is None or c_lon is None:
        return {
            'is_valid': True,
            'is_inside': True,
            'distance_meters': 0.0,
            'allowed_radius': radius,
            'reason': 'Campus boundary not yet configured; check allowed.',
        }

    distance = calculate_distance_meters(u_lat, u_lon, c_lat, c_lon)

    try:
        drift_buffer = min(max(float(accuracy_meters or 0), 0.0), 35.0)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        drift_buffer = 0.0

    is_inside = distance <= (radius + drift_buffer)
    return {
        'is_valid': True,
        'is_inside': is_inside,
        'distance_meters': round(distance, 1),
        'allowed_radius': radius,
        'reason': 'Within campus boundary.' if is_inside else f'Outside campus boundary ({round(distance)}m away; limit is {radius}m).',
    }


def is_within_campus_geofence(latitude, longitude, accuracy_meters=None, is_mock_location=False) -> bool:
    """Boolean shortcut for campus GPS verification."""
    return verify_campus_gps(latitude, longitude, accuracy_meters, is_mock_location)['is_inside']


def get_client_ip(request) -> str:
    """Safely extracts the client IP from reverse proxy headers or local network."""
    if not request:
        return '127.0.0.1'
    cf_ip = request.META.get('HTTP_CF_CONNECTING_IP')
    if cf_ip:
        return cf_ip.strip()
    xff = request.META.get('HTTP_X_FORWARDED_FOR')
    if xff:
        return xff.split(',')[0].strip()
    return request.META.get('REMOTE_ADDR', '127.0.0.1')


class IsAcademicManagerPermission(permissions.BasePermission):
    """
    Allows authenticated users to view academic records, while restricting
    create/edit/delete changes to school leadership and registrars.
    """
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        if not request.user or not request.user.is_authenticated:
            return False
        if request.method in permissions.SAFE_METHODS:
            return True
        return is_leadership_role(request.user) or get_user_role(request.user) == 'REGISTRAR'


class IsSystemAdminRole(permissions.BasePermission):
    """Restricts access strictly to System Administrators and Superusers."""
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        if not request.user or not request.user.is_authenticated:
            return False
        return bool(
            request.user.is_superuser
            or get_user_role(request.user) in {'ADMIN', 'SUPERADMIN'}
        )


IsLeadershipOrReadOnly = IsAcademicManagerPermission
IsAdminOrReadOnly = IsAcademicManagerPermission
IsLeadershipUser = IsSystemAdminRole
IsSchoolAdmin = IsSystemAdminRole
IsPrincipalOrAdmin = IsAcademicManagerPermission


class IsFacultyOrLeadership(permissions.BasePermission):
    """Allows access to verified faculty, advisers, and school leadership."""
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        if not request.user or not request.user.is_authenticated:
            return False
        if is_leadership_role(request.user):
            return True
        return resolve_faculty_for_user(request.user) is not None


IsFacultyUser = IsFacultyOrLeadership


def get_active_academic_year():
    """Returns the currently active AcademicYear or the latest record."""
    from apps.academics.models import AcademicYear
    return AcademicYear.objects.filter(is_active=True).first() or AcademicYear.objects.first()


def get_faculty_section_ids(faculty) -> set:
    if not faculty:
        return set()
    from apps.academics.models import Schedule, Section
    advised_ids = set(Section.objects.filter(adviser=faculty).values_list('id', flat=True))
    teaching_ids = set(Schedule.objects.filter(faculty=faculty).values_list('section_id', flat=True))
    return advised_ids | teaching_ids


def get_faculty_student_ids(faculty) -> set:
    section_ids = get_faculty_section_ids(faculty)
    if not section_ids:
        return set()
    from apps.academics.models import Enrollment
    return set(
        Enrollment.objects.filter(
            section_id__in=section_ids,
            student__is_active=True,
        )
        .exclude(status__in=['DROPPED', 'TRANSFERRED_OUT', 'INACTIVE'])
        .values_list('student_id', flat=True)
        .distinct()
    )


def get_student_active_section(student):
    if not student:
        return None
    from apps.academics.models import Enrollment
    enr = (
        Enrollment.objects.filter(student=student)
        .exclude(status__in=['DROPPED', 'TRANSFERRED_OUT', 'INACTIVE'])
        .select_related('section', 'section__grade_level')
        .order_by('-id')
        .first()
    )
    return enr.section if enr else None


def get_school_metadata(request=None) -> dict:
    from apps.academics.models import SchoolProfile
    school = SchoolProfile.objects.select_related('principal_faculty').first()

    if not school:
        return {
            'school_id': '',
            'school_name': '',
            'region': '',
            'division': '',
            'district': '',
            'academic_year': '',
            'school_address': '',
            'school_contact': '',
            'school_email': '',
            'website': '',
            'school_head': '',
            'principal_title': '',
            'superintendent_name': '',
            'left_logo': None,
            'right_logo': None,
            'school_logo': None,
            'is_configured': False,
        }

    school_head = str(school.principal_name or '').strip()
    if not school_head and school.principal_faculty:
        school_head = getattr(school.principal_faculty, 'full_name', str(school.principal_faculty))

    return {
        'school_id': str(school.school_id or '').strip(),
        'school_name': str(school.school_name or '').strip(),
        'region': str(school.region or '').strip(),
        'division': str(school.division or '').strip(),
        'district': str(school.district or '').strip(),
        'academic_year': str(getattr(school, 'academic_year', '') or '').strip(),
        'school_address': str(school.address or '').strip(),
        'school_contact': str(school.contact_number or '').strip(),
        'school_email': str(school.email or '').strip(),
        'website': str(getattr(school, 'website', '') or '').strip(),
        'school_head': school_head.upper() if school_head else '',
        'principal_title': str(school.principal_title or '').strip(),
        'superintendent_name': str(getattr(school, 'superintendent_name', '') or '').strip(),
        'left_logo': resolve_logo_field(school.left_logo, request),
        'right_logo': resolve_logo_field(school.right_logo, request),
        'school_logo': resolve_logo_field(school.school_logo, request),
        'is_configured': bool(school.school_name and school.school_id),
    }

"""
AttendSure - Live Classroom & Mobile App Dashboard API (No Hardware / No push)
File: backend/apps/api/views/dashboard.py
"""

import time as time_module
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from django.utils import timezone
from django.conf import settings
from django.core.cache import cache
from rest_framework.views import APIView
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework import permissions

from apps.academics.models import (
    AppNotification,
    DailyAttendanceSummary,
    Enrollment,
    FacultyHeartbeat,
    FacultyProfile,
    Student,
    SubjectAttendanceLog,
    UserProfile,
)
from .common import (
    get_user_role,
    is_regular_faculty,
    resolve_faculty_for_user,
    get_faculty_student_ids,
    format_full_student_name,
    format_deduped_section_label,
    safe_photo_url,
)


class DashboardOverviewAPIView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        query_start = time_module.perf_counter()
        user_role = get_user_role(request.user)
        is_faculty_only = is_regular_faculty(user_role) and not request.user.is_superuser

        cache_key = f"dashboard_overview_faculty_{request.user.id}" if is_faculty_only else "dashboard_overview_admin"
        cached_data = cache.get(cache_key)
        if cached_data is not None:
            cached_data['analytics']['average_latency_ms'] = max(1, round((time_module.perf_counter() - query_start) * 1000))
            return Response(cached_data, status=200)

        today = timezone.localdate()
        today_start = timezone.make_aware(datetime.combine(today, datetime.min.time()))
        today_end = timezone.make_aware(datetime.combine(today, datetime.max.time()))

        if is_faculty_only:
            user_faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(request.user)
            assigned_student_ids = get_faculty_student_ids(user_faculty)
            total_students = len(assigned_student_ids)

            present_ids = set(
                DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.filter(
                    attendance_date=today,
                    student_id__in=assigned_student_ids,
                    status__in=['PRESENT', 'LATE'],
                ).values_list('student_id', flat=True)
            )
            cutting_count = DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.filter(
                attendance_date=today,
                student_id__in=assigned_student_ids,
                status='CUTTING',
            ).count()

            recent_logs = (
                SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(
                    attendance_date=today,
                    student_id__in=assigned_student_ids,
                )
                .select_related('student', 'schedule__subject')
                .order_by('-scanned_at')[:6]
            )
            total_faculty = 1
            present_faculty = 1 if (user_faculty and FacultyHeartbeat.objects.filter(
                faculty=user_faculty,
                recorded_at__gte=today_start,
                is_inside_geofence=True,
            ).exists()) else 0
            push_alerts_today = AppNotification.objects.filter(
                student_id__in=assigned_student_ids,
                created_at__gte=today_start,
                created_at__lte=today_end,
            ).count()
            mobile_parents_count = Student.objects.filter(
                id__in=assigned_student_ids,
                parent_user__isnull=False,
            ).values('parent_user_id').distinct().count()
        else:
            total_students = Student.objects.filter(is_active=True).count()
            present_ids = set(
                DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.filter(
                    attendance_date=today,
                    status__in=['PRESENT', 'LATE'],
                ).values_list('student_id', flat=True)
            )
            cutting_count = DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.filter(
                attendance_date=today,
                status='CUTTING',
            ).count()

            total_faculty = FacultyProfile.objects.filter(is_active=True).count()
            present_faculty = FacultyHeartbeat.objects.filter(
                recorded_at__gte=today_start,
                is_inside_geofence=True,
            ).values('faculty_id').distinct().count()

            recent_logs = (
                SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(attendance_date=today)
                .select_related('student', 'schedule__subject')
                .order_by('-scanned_at')[:6]
            )
            push_alerts_today = AppNotification.objects.filter(
                created_at__gte=today_start,
                created_at__lte=today_end,
            ).count()
            mobile_parents_count = UserProfile.objects.filter(
                role='PARENT',
                user__is_active=True,
            ).count()

        students_present = len(present_ids)
        absent_students = max(0, total_students - students_present)
        attendance_rate = round((students_present / total_students * 100), 1) if total_students > 0 else 0.0

        hour_counts = {h: 0 for h in range(6, 18)}
        for scan_dt in SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(attendance_date=today).values_list('scanned_at', flat=True):
            if scan_dt:
                local_h = timezone.localtime(scan_dt).hour
                if local_h in hour_counts:
                    hour_counts[local_h] += 1

        max_hour = max(hour_counts.values() or [1])
        hourly_scans = [
            {
                'hour': f"{h % 12 or 12} {'AM' if h < 12 else 'PM'}",
                'count': count,
                'height': round((count / max_hour * 100)) if max_hour else 0,
                'is_peak': count == max_hour and count > 0,
            }
            for h, count in hour_counts.items()
        ]

        recent_student_ids = [l.student_id for l in recent_logs]
        enrollments_map = {
            enr.student_id: enr
            for enr in Enrollment.objects.filter(
                student_id__in=recent_student_ids,
                status='ENROLLED',
            ).select_related('section', 'section__grade_level')
        }

        recent_scans_data = []
        for log in recent_logs:
            enr = enrollments_map.get(log.student_id)
            grade_code = enr.section.grade_level.code if (enr and enr.section and enr.section.grade_level) else ""
            section_name = enr.section.name if (enr and enr.section) else ""
            subject_code = log.schedule.subject.code if (log.schedule and log.schedule.subject) else "CLASS"

            recent_scans_data.append({
                'id': log.id,
                'person_name': format_full_student_name(log.student),
                'photo_url': safe_photo_url(log.student.photo, request),
                'status': log.status,
                'subject_code': subject_code,
                'grade_section': format_deduped_section_label(grade_code, section_name),
                'scan_time': timezone.localtime(log.scanned_at).strftime('%I:%M:%S %p') if log.scanned_at else '',
            })

        latency_ms = max(1, round((time_module.perf_counter() - query_start) * 1000))

        response_data = {
            'total_students': total_students,
            'students_present': students_present,
            'students_on_campus': students_present,
            'students_absent': absent_students,
            'students_cutting': cutting_count,
            'attendance_rate': attendance_rate,
            'faculty_on_duty': present_faculty,
            'total_faculty': total_faculty,
            'classroom_scans_today': sum(hour_counts.values()),
            'push_alerts_today': push_alerts_today,
            'mobile_parents_connected': mobile_parents_count,
            'hourly_scans': hourly_scans,
            'recent_scans': recent_scans_data,
            'analytics': {'average_latency_ms': latency_ms},
            'database': {'status': 'CONNECTED', 'database_name': settings.DATABASES['default']['NAME']},
        }

        cache.set(cache_key, response_data, timeout=15)
        return Response(response_data, status=200)
