class SARDOEarlyWarningAPIView(APIView):
    """
    Analyzes 30-day attendance logs to flag learners at risk of dropping out (SARDO).
    """
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        user = request.user
        today = timezone.localdate()
        window_start = today - timedelta(days=30)

        enrollments_qs = (
            Enrollment.objects.filter(status='ENROLLED', student__is_active=True)
            .select_related('student', 'section', 'section__grade_level')
        )

        if not (user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(user)):
            faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
            if not faculty:
                return Response(
                    {'at_risk_students': [], 'critical_sardo_count': 0, 'early_warning_count': 0},
                    status=status.HTTP_200_OK,
                )
            enrollments_qs = enrollments_qs.filter(section_id__in=get_faculty_section_ids(faculty))

        section_id = request.query_params.get('section_id')
        if section_id and str(section_id).isdigit():
            enrollments_qs = enrollments_qs.filter(section_id=int(section_id))

        enrollments = list(enrollments_qs)
        student_ids = [e.student_id for e in enrollments]

        stats_by_student = {
            row['student_id']: row
            for row in DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.filter(
                student_id__in=student_ids,
                attendance_date__gte=window_start,
                attendance_date__lte=today,
            )
            .values('student_id')
            .annotate(
                absent_days=Count('id', filter=Q(status='ABSENT')),
                cutting_days=Count('id', filter=Q(status='CUTTING')),
                late_days=Count('id', filter=Q(status='LATE')),
            )
        }

        morning_lates = dict(
            SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(
                student_id__in=student_ids,
                attendance_date__gte=window_start,
                status='LATE',
                schedule__start_time__lte=time(8, 30),
            )
            .values('student_id')
            .annotate(cnt=Count('id'))
            .values_list('student_id', 'cnt')
        )

        at_risk_list = []
        critical_count = 0
        warning_count = 0

        for enr in enrollments:
            st = enr.student
            row = stats_by_student.get(st.id, {})
            absences = row.get('absent_days', 0)
            cutting = row.get('cutting_days', 0)
            lates = row.get('late_days', 0)
            first_period_lates = morning_lates.get(st.id, 0)

            risk_score = min(100, (absences * 15) + (cutting * 20) + (lates * 5) + (first_period_lates * 3))
            if risk_score < 20:
                continue

            if absences >= 5 or cutting >= 3 or risk_score >= 60:
                risk_level = 'CRITICAL_SARDO'
                action_needed = 'Immediate parent conference and home visitation required.'
                critical_count += 1
            else:
                risk_level = 'EARLY_WARNING'
                action_needed = (
                    'Chronic morning lateness detected; check transport or nutrition support.'
                    if first_period_lates >= 3
                    else 'Adviser counseling and mobile app parent follow-up recommended.'
                )
                warning_count += 1

            sec = enr.section
            grade_name = sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else ''

            at_risk_list.append({
                'student_id': st.id,
                'lrn': st.lrn,
                'full_name': format_full_student_name(st),
                'sex': st.sex,
                'section': format_deduped_section_label(grade_name, sec.name if sec else ''),
                'is_4ps_beneficiary': enr.is_cct_recipient,
                'parent_contact': st.parent_contact,
                'parent_app_linked': bool(st.parent_user_id),
                'photo_url': safe_photo_url(st.photo, request),
                'absent_days_30d': absences,
                'cutting_days_30d': cutting,
                'late_days_30d': lates,
                'risk_score': risk_score,
                'risk_level': risk_level,
                'recommended_intervention': action_needed,
            })

        at_risk_list.sort(key=lambda item: item['risk_score'], reverse=True)

        return Response({
            'window_days': 30,
            'total_evaluated': len(enrollments),
            'critical_sardo_count': critical_count,
            'early_warning_count': warning_count,
            'at_risk_students': at_risk_list[:100],
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EmergencyCampusRollCallAPIView(APIView):
    """Provides an instant live campus headcount of present students and faculty."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        today = timezone.localdate()
        now_dt = timezone.now()

        departed_student_ids = set(
            GatePass.objects.filter(
                student__isnull=False,
                valid_from__date=today,
                status__in=['ACTIVE', 'USED'],
            ).values_list('student_id', flat=True)
        )

        present_summaries = (
            DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.filter(
                attendance_date=today,
                status__in=['PRESENT', 'LATE'],
            )
            .exclude(student_id__in=departed_student_ids)
            .select_related('student', 'section', 'section__grade_level')
            .order_by('section__grade_level__level_order', 'section__name', 'student__last_name')
        )

        students_on_campus = []
        for item in present_summaries:
            st = item.student
            sec = item.section
            grade_name = sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else ''
            students_on_campus.append({
                'student_id': st.id,
                'lrn': st.lrn,
                'full_name': format_full_student_name(st),
                'section': format_deduped_section_label(grade_name, sec.name if sec else ''),
                'parent_contact': st.parent_contact,
                'photo_url': safe_photo_url(st.photo, request),
            })

        faculty_ids = (
            FacultyHeartbeat.objects.filter(recorded_at__date=today, is_inside_geofence=True)
            .values_list('faculty_id', flat=True)
            .distinct()
        )
        faculty_on_campus = [
            {
                'faculty_id': f.id,
                'employee_id': f.employee_id,
                'full_name': f.full_name,
                'department': f.department,
            }
            for f in FacultyProfile.objects.filter(id__in=faculty_ids, is_active=True).order_by('last_name')
        ]

        return Response({
            'timestamp': timezone.localtime(now_dt).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %I:%M:%S %p'),
            'total_people_on_campus': len(students_on_campus) + len(faculty_on_campus),
            'students_count': len(students_on_campus),
            'faculty_count': len(faculty_on_campus),
            'students': students_on_campus,
            'faculty': faculty_on_campus,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EdFiOpenStandardExportAPIView(APIView):
    """Exports standardized student attendance events for LMS and ministry reporting."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        target_date_str = request.query_params.get('date')
        target_date = timezone.localdate()
        if target_date_str:
            try:
                target_date = timezone.datetime.strptime(target_date_str, '%Y-%m-%d').date()
            except ValueError:
                pass

        school = get_school_metadata(request)
        logs = (
            SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(attendance_date=target_date)
            .select_related('student', 'schedule__section', 'schedule__subject', 'faculty')
            .order_by('scanned_at')[:1000]
        )

        events = [
            {
                'id': f"att-{log.id}",
                'schoolId': school['school_id'],
                'eventDate': str(log.attendance_date),
                'studentReference': {
                    'studentUniqueId': log.student.lrn,
                    'fullName': format_full_student_name(log.student),
                },
                'sectionReference': {
                    'sectionIdentifier': log.schedule.section.name if (log.schedule and log.schedule.section) else '',
                    'courseCode': log.schedule.subject.code if (log.schedule and log.schedule.subject) else '',
                },
                'attendanceEventCategoryDescriptor': f"uri://ed-fi.org/AttendanceEventCategoryDescriptor#{log.status.title()}",
                'captureMethod': 'USB_QR_SCANNER_PC',
                'recordedAt': timezone.localtime(log.scanned_at).isoformat() if log.scanned_at else '',
            }
            for log in logs
        ]

        return Response({
            'standard': 'Ed-Fi v6.1 AttendanceEvent',
            'school': school['school_name'],
            'date': str(target_date),
            'total_events': len(events),
            'events': events,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class EnterpriseSDGImpactAPIView(APIView):
    """Computes live institutional SDG metrics from real PostgreSQL records."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        total_students = Student.objects.filter(is_active=True).count()
        linked_parents = Student.objects.filter(is_active=True, parent_user__isnull=False).count()
        total_push_alerts = AppNotification.objects.count()
        active_sections = Section.objects.filter(is_active=True).count()
        active_schedules = Schedule.objects.filter(is_active=True).count()
        active_parents = UserProfile.objects.filter(role='PARENT', user__is_active=True).count()

        parent_coverage_pct = round((linked_parents / total_students * 100), 1) if total_students > 0 else 0.0

        return Response({
            'status': 'success',
            'metrics': {
                'total_active_students': total_students,
                'linked_parent_students': linked_parents,
                'parent_app_coverage_percent': parent_coverage_pct,
                'active_parent_accounts': active_parents,
                'total_push_alerts_sent': total_push_alerts,
                'active_sections': active_sections,
                'active_schedules': active_schedules,
            },
            'sdg_alignment': {
                'goal_4': 'Quality Education - Live USB QR attendance and SARDO early warning',
                'goal_8': 'Decent Work - Mobile facial recognition & campus GPS check-in for faculty',
                'goal_16': 'Strong Institutions - Tamper-evident digital audit logs and real-time parent push alerts',
            },
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

"""
AttendSure - Digital Exit Pass Management (Mobile Push Enabled, No Hardware/push)
File: backend/apps/api/views/gate.py
"""

import uuid
from datetime import timedelta
from django.utils import timezone
from django.db.models import Q
from rest_framework import serializers, viewsets, status, permissions
from rest_framework.decorators import action
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.parsers import MultiPartParser, FormParser, JSONParser

from apps.academics.models import Student, FacultyProfile, GatePass
from apps.api.services.push_service import notify_student_parent
from .common import (
    get_user_role,
    is_leadership_role,
    resolve_faculty_for_user,
    get_faculty_student_ids,
)

ALLOWED_DOCUMENT_EXTENSIONS = ('.pdf', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png', '.webp')
MAX_DOCUMENT_SIZE_BYTES = 10 * 1024 * 1024


class GatePassSerializer(serializers.ModelSerializer):
    student_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField(read_only=True)
    student_lrn = serializers.SerializerMethodField(read_only=True)
    faculty_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField(read_only=True)
    adviser_name = serializers.SerializerMethodField(read_only=True)

    attachment = serializers.FileField(required=False, allow_null=True)
    pass_type = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default='OFFICIAL_BUSINESS')
    reason = serializers.CharField(required=False, allow_blank=True, default='Official campus business')

    student = serializers.PrimaryKeyRelatedField(queryset=Student.objects.all(), required=False, allow_null=True)
    faculty = serializers.PrimaryKeyRelatedField(queryset=FacultyProfile.objects.all(), required=False, allow_null=True)

    class Meta:
        model = GatePass
        fields = [
            'id', 'pass_number', 'student', 'student_name', 'student_lrn',
            'faculty', 'faculty_name', 'pass_type', 'reason',
            'status', 'valid_from', 'valid_to', 'attachment',
            'issued_by', 'adviser_name', 'created_at', 'updated_at',
        ]
        read_only_fields = ['id', 'pass_number', 'status', 'issued_by', 'adviser_name', 'created_at', 'updated_at']

    def get_student_name(self, obj):
        return obj.student.full_name if getattr(obj, 'student', None) else None

    def get_student_lrn(self, obj):
        return obj.student.lrn if getattr(obj, 'student', None) else None

    def get_faculty_name(self, obj):
        return obj.faculty.full_name if getattr(obj, 'faculty', None) else None

    def get_adviser_name(self, obj):
        return obj.issued_by.full_name if getattr(obj, 'issued_by', None) else 'Adviser / Faculty'

    def validate(self, attrs):
        if self.instance is None and not attrs.get('student') and not attrs.get('faculty'):
            raise serializers.ValidationError({'detail': 'Please select a student or faculty member for this pass.'})

        attachment = attrs.get('attachment')
        if attachment and hasattr(attachment, 'name'):
            if not attachment.name.lower().endswith(ALLOWED_DOCUMENT_EXTENSIONS):
                raise serializers.ValidationError({'attachment': 'Only PDF, JPG, PNG, and WebP files are allowed.'})
            if attachment.size > MAX_DOCUMENT_SIZE_BYTES:
                raise serializers.ValidationError({'attachment': 'Document size exceeds the 10 MB limit.'})

        valid_from = attrs.get('valid_from') or (self.instance.valid_from if self.instance else timezone.now())
        valid_to = attrs.get('valid_to') or (self.instance.valid_to if self.instance else None)
        if valid_to and valid_to <= valid_from:
            raise serializers.ValidationError({'valid_to': 'Expiration time must be later than departure time.'})

        return attrs


class GatePassViewSet(viewsets.ModelViewSet):
    """Manages official campus exit passes and notifies parent mobile apps."""
    queryset = GatePass.objects.select_related('student', 'faculty', 'issued_by').all().order_by('-created_at')
    serializer_class = GatePassSerializer
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]
    parser_classes = [MultiPartParser, FormParser, JSONParser]

    def get_queryset(self):
        user = self.request.user
        role = get_user_role(user)
        qs = self.queryset

        status_param = self.request.query_params.get('status', 'ALL').strip().upper()
        if status_param != 'ALL':
            qs = qs.filter(status=status_param)

        search = self.request.query_params.get('search', '').strip()
        if search:
            qs = qs.filter(
                Q(pass_number__icontains=search) |
                Q(student__first_name__icontains=search) |
                Q(student__last_name__icontains=search) |
                Q(student__lrn__icontains=search) |
                Q(faculty__first_name__icontains=search) |
                Q(faculty__last_name__icontains=search) |
                Q(reason__icontains=search)
            )

        if role == 'PARENT':
            qs = qs.filter(student__parent_user=user)
        elif role == 'FACULTY' and not (user.is_superuser or user.is_staff):
            fac = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
            if fac:
                allowed_student_ids = get_faculty_student_ids(fac)
                qs = qs.filter(
                    Q(faculty_id=fac.id) |
                    Q(issued_by_id=fac.id) |
                    Q(student_id__in=allowed_student_ids)
                )
            else:
                qs = qs.none()

        return qs

    def list(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        queryset = self.filter_queryset(self.get_queryset())[:300]
        page = self.paginate_queryset(queryset)
        if page is not None:
            serializer = self.get_serializer(page, many=True)
            return self.get_paginated_response(serializer.data)
        serializer = self.get_serializer(queryset, many=True)
        return Response(serializer.data)

    def _can_modify_pass(self, user, gate_pass) -> bool:
        if user.is_superuser or user.is_staff or is_leadership_role(user):
            return True
        fac = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if not fac:
            return False
        return gate_pass.faculty_id == fac.id or gate_pass.issued_by_id == fac.id

    def perform_update(self, serializer):
        if not self._can_modify_pass(self.request.user, serializer.instance):
            raise permissions.PermissionDenied('You do not have permission to edit this exit pass.')
        serializer.save()

    def perform_destroy(self, instance):
        if not self._can_modify_pass(self.request.user, instance):
            raise permissions.PermissionDenied('You do not have permission to delete this exit pass.')
        instance.delete()

    def create(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        data = request.data.dict() if hasattr(request.data, 'dict') else dict(request.data)
        if request.FILES.get('attachment'):
            data['attachment'] = request.FILES.get('attachment')

        user = request.user
        is_leader = is_leadership_role(user) or user.is_superuser

        data.setdefault('pass_type', data.get('type') or 'OFFICIAL_BUSINESS')
        data.setdefault('reason', data.get('details') or data['pass_type'])
        if 'student_id' in data and not data.get('student'):
            data['student'] = data.get('student_id')
        if 'faculty_id' in data and not data.get('faculty'):
            data['faculty'] = data.get('faculty_id')

        now = timezone.now()
        data.setdefault('valid_from', now.isoformat())
        data.setdefault('valid_to', (now + timedelta(hours=4)).isoformat())

        fac = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if not fac:
            fac = FacultyProfile.objects.filter(is_active=True).order_by('id').first()

        is_student_pass = bool(data.get('student'))
        if not is_student_pass and not data.get('faculty') and fac:
            data['faculty'] = fac.id

        initial_status = 'ACTIVE' if (is_leader or is_student_pass) else 'PENDING'
        pass_code = f"GP-{timezone.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()}"
        data['pass_number'] = pass_code

        serializer = self.get_serializer(data=data)
        if not serializer.is_valid():
            return Response(serializer.errors, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        pass_obj = serializer.save(pass_number=pass_code, status=initial_status, issued_by=fac)

        if is_student_pass and pass_obj.student:
            adviser_name = fac.full_name if fac else (user.get_full_name() or user.username)
            notify_student_parent(
                student=pass_obj.student,
                title=f'Campus Exit Pass Issued: {pass_obj.student.first_name}',
                message=(
                    f'Adviser {adviser_name} issued exit pass #{pass_code} for '
                    f'{pass_obj.student.full_name}. Reason: {pass_obj.reason}.'
                ),
                category='GATE_PASS',
                extra_data={'pass_number': pass_code},
            )

        return Response(GatePassSerializer(pass_obj, context={'request': request}).data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='approve')
    def approve_pass(self, request, pk=None):
        if not (self.request.user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(self.request.user)):
            return Response({'error': 'Only school leadership can approve passes.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)
        gate_pass = self.get_object()
        gate_pass.status = 'ACTIVE'
        gate_pass.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])
        return Response({'success': True, 'status': 'ACTIVE'}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='decline')
    def decline_pass(self, request, pk=None):
        if not (self.request.user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(self.request.user)):
            return Response({'error': 'Only school leadership can decline passes.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)
        gate_pass = self.get_object()
        gate_pass.status = 'REVOKED'
        gate_pass.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])
        return Response({'success': True, 'status': 'REVOKED'}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @action(detail=True, methods=['post'], url_path='mark-used')
    def mark_used(self, request, pk=None):
        gate_pass = self.get_object()
        if gate_pass.status != 'ACTIVE':
            return Response({'error': f'Pass status is {gate_pass.status}.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        now = timezone.now()
        if now > gate_pass.valid_to:
            gate_pass.status = 'EXPIRED'
            gate_pass.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])
            return Response({'error': 'Exit pass has expired.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        gate_pass.status = 'USED'
        gate_pass.save(update_fields=['status', 'updated_at'])

        if gate_pass.student:
            time_str = timezone.localtime(now).strftime('%I:%M %p')
            notify_student_parent(
                student=gate_pass.student,
                title=f'Authorized Campus Departure: {gate_pass.student.first_name}',
                message=f'{gate_pass.student.full_name} departed campus on an approved exit pass ({gate_pass.reason}) at {time_str}.',
                category='GATE_PASS',
            )

        return Response({'success': True, 'status': 'USED'}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

"""
AttendSure - Faculty Mobile Facial Recognition, Campus GPS Check-In & Device Binding
File: backend/apps/api/views/geofence.py
"""

from datetime import timedelta
from django.db import transaction
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.academics.models import (
    AuditLog,
    FacultyHeartbeat,
    FacultyProfile,
    GeofenceSetting,
    LoafingIncident,
    Schedule,
    SchoolProfile,
)
from apps.api.serializers import FacultyMobileCheckInSerializer
from .common import (
    IsAcademicManagerPermission,
    get_client_ip,
    is_leadership_role,
    resolve_faculty_for_user,
    verify_campus_gps,
)


def _fetch_geofence_payload(request) -> dict:
    """Fetches real campus GPS boundary settings and today's faculty mobile facial check-ins."""
    user = request.user
    today = timezone.localdate()

    geofence = GeofenceSetting.objects.filter(is_active=True).first()
    school = SchoolProfile.objects.first()

    lat = (
        float(geofence.latitude)
        if (geofence and geofence.latitude is not None)
        else (float(school.latitude) if (school and school.latitude is not None) else 8.4858)
    )
    lon = (
        float(geofence.longitude)
        if (geofence and geofence.longitude is not None)
        else (float(school.longitude) if (school and school.longitude is not None) else 124.6567)
    )
    radius = (
        int(geofence.radius_meters)
        if (geofence and geofence.radius_meters)
        else (int(school.geofence_radius_meters) if (school and school.geofence_radius_meters) else 150)
    )
    name = geofence.name if geofence else (school.school_name if school else 'Campus Boundary')

    if user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(user):
        heartbeats = (
            FacultyHeartbeat.objects.filter(recorded_at__date=today)
            .select_related('faculty')
            .order_by('-recorded_at')[:100]
        )
        loafing = (
            LoafingIncident.objects.filter(incident_date=today)
            .select_related('faculty')
            .order_by('-created_at')
        )
    else:
        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
        if not faculty:
            heartbeats, loafing = [], []
        else:
            heartbeats = (
                FacultyHeartbeat.objects.filter(faculty=faculty, recorded_at__date=today)
                .select_related('faculty')
                .order_by('-recorded_at')[:50]
            )
            loafing = (
                LoafingIncident.objects.filter(faculty=faculty, incident_date=today)
                .select_related('faculty')
                .order_by('-created_at')
            )

    return {
        'id': geofence.id if geofence else (school.id if school else 1),
        'name': name,
        'latitude': lat,
        'longitude': lon,
        'radius_meters': radius,
        'geofence_radius_meters': radius,
        'is_active': geofence.is_active if geofence else True,
        'heartbeats': [
            {
                'id': hb.id,
                'faculty_id': hb.faculty_id,
                'faculty_name': hb.faculty.full_name,
                'latitude': float(hb.latitude),
                'longitude': float(hb.longitude),
                'battery_level': hb.battery_level,
                'is_inside_geofence': hb.is_inside_geofence,
                'verification_method': 'MOBILE_FACE_AND_GPS',
                'recorded_at': timezone.localtime(hb.recorded_at).strftime('%I:%M:%S %p'),
            }
            for hb in heartbeats
        ],
        'loafing_incidents': [
            {
                'id': inc.id,
                'faculty_name': inc.faculty.full_name,
                'trigger_reason': inc.trigger_reason,
                'status': inc.status,
                'created_at': timezone.localtime(inc.created_at).strftime('%I:%M %p'),
            }
            for inc in loafing
        ],
    }


class GeofenceManagementAPIView(APIView):
    """Reads and updates campus GPS geofence boundaries from a faculty admin laptop/PC."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsAcademicManagerPermission]

    def get(self, request):
        return Response(_fetch_geofence_payload(request), status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

    @transaction.atomic
    def post(self, request):
        return self._save_geofence(request)

    @transaction.atomic
    def put(self, request):
        return self._save_geofence(request)

    @transaction.atomic
    def patch(self, request):
        return self._save_geofence(request)

    def _save_geofence(self, request):
        data = request.data or {}
        raw_lat = data.get('latitude')
        raw_lon = data.get('longitude')
        raw_rad = data.get('radius_meters') or data.get('geofence_radius_meters')
        name = str(data.get('name', 'Campus Boundary')).strip() or 'Campus Boundary'

        try:
            lat = float(raw_lat)
            lon = float(raw_lon)
            if not (-90.0 <= lat <= 90.0) or not (-180.0 <= lon <= 180.0):
                raise ValueError('Out of bounds')
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Valid latitude (-90 to 90) and longitude (-180 to 180) are required.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        try:
            radius = int(raw_rad) if raw_rad is not None else 150
            if not (20 <= radius <= 5000):
                return Response(
                    {'error': 'Radius must be between 20 and 5000 meters.'},
                    status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                )
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Radius must be a valid integer in meters.'},
                status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            )

        geofence = GeofenceSetting.objects.filter(is_active=True).first() or GeofenceSetting.objects.first()
        if geofence:
            geofence.name = name
            geofence.latitude = lat
            geofence.longitude = lon
            geofence.radius_meters = radius
            geofence.is_active = bool(data.get('is_active', True))
            geofence.save()
        else:
            geofence = GeofenceSetting.objects.create(
                name=name,
                latitude=lat,
                longitude=lon,
                radius_meters=radius,
                is_active=True,
            )

        school = SchoolProfile.objects.first()
        if school:
            school.latitude = lat
            school.longitude = lon
            school.geofence_radius_meters = radius
            school.save(update_fields=['latitude', 'longitude', 'geofence_radius_meters', 'updated_at'])

        AuditLog.objects.create(
            table_name='geofence_settings',
            record_id=geofence.id,
            action='UPDATE',
            new_data={'latitude': lat, 'longitude': lon, 'radius_meters': radius},
            performed_by=request.user,
            client_ip=get_client_ip(request),
            user_agent=str(request.META.get('HTTP_USER_AGENT', ''))[:255],
        )

        return Response(_fetch_geofence_payload(request), status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class GeofenceStatusAPIView(APIView):
    """Returns current campus GPS status and recent faculty mobile check-ins."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        payload = _fetch_geofence_payload(request)
        return Response({
            'heartbeats': payload['heartbeats'],
            'loafing_incidents': payload['loafing_incidents'],
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class GeofenceAuditAPIView(APIView):
    """Returns real GPS check-in logs, loafing incidents, and boundary audit history."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        user = request.user
        is_admin = bool(user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(user))
        payload = _fetch_geofence_payload(request)

        audit_qs = AuditLog.objects.select_related('performed_by').filter(
            table_name__in=['geofence_settings', 'attendsure_school_profile', 'faculty_heartbeats', 'loafing_incidents']
        ).order_by('-created_at')
        if not is_admin:
            audit_qs = audit_qs.filter(performed_by=user)

        audit_entries = []
        for entry in audit_qs[:50]:
            actor = (
                (entry.performed_by.get_full_name() or entry.performed_by.username)
                if entry.performed_by
                else 'System'
            )
            local_dt = timezone.localtime(entry.created_at)
            audit_entries.append({
                'id': entry.id,
                'user': actor,
                'action': entry.action,
                'table_name': entry.table_name,
                'details': f'Boundary configuration {entry.action.lower()} (#{entry.record_id})',
                'ip_address': entry.client_ip or '127.0.0.1',
                'timestamp': local_dt.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %I:%M:%S %p'),
            })

        records = [
            {
                'id': f"hb-{hb['id']}",
                'faculty_id': hb['faculty_id'],
                'faculty_name': hb['faculty_name'],
                'latitude': hb['latitude'],
                'longitude': hb['longitude'],
                'battery_level': hb['battery_level'],
                'is_inside_geofence': hb['is_inside_geofence'],
                'status': 'INSIDE_CAMPUS' if hb['is_inside_geofence'] else 'OUTSIDE_CAMPUS',
                'timestamp': hb['recorded_at'],
            }
            for hb in payload['heartbeats']
        ]

        return Response({
            'success': True,
            'count': len(records),
            'results': records,
            'records': records,
            'heartbeats': payload['heartbeats'],
            'loafing_incidents': payload['loafing_incidents'],
            'audit_logs': audit_entries,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class GeofenceCheckInAPIView(APIView):
    """
    Records a faculty member's attendance via Mobile Facial Recognition + Liveness + Campus GPS,
    enforces 1-to-1 mobile phone binding, and flags missed class scans.
    """
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request):
        faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(request.user)
        if not faculty:
            return Response({'error': 'No faculty profile linked to this account.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        serializer = FacultyMobileCheckInSerializer(data=request.data)
        if not serializer.is_valid():
            return Response(serializer.errors, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        data = serializer.validated_data
        if data.get('face_verified') is False or data.get('liveness_passed') is False:
            return Response(
                {'error': 'Mobile facial recognition or liveness check failed. Please align your face in good lighting.'},
                status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            )

        device_id = str(data.get('client_device_id') or request.headers.get('X-Device-UUID') or '').strip()
        if device_id:
            if not faculty.bound_device_id:
                faculty.bound_device_id = device_id
                faculty.device_bound_at = timezone.now()
                faculty.save(update_fields=['bound_device_id', 'device_bound_at'])
            elif faculty.bound_device_id != device_id:
                return Response(
                    {'error': 'This account is bound to another mobile phone. Ask an administrator to reset device binding.'},
                    status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                )

        gps = verify_campus_gps(
            latitude=data['latitude'],
            longitude=data['longitude'],
            accuracy_meters=data.get('accuracy'),
            is_mock_location=data.get('is_mock_location', False),
        )
        if not gps['is_valid']:
            return Response({'error': gps['reason']}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        now = timezone.now()
        hb = FacultyHeartbeat.objects.create(
            faculty=faculty,
            latitude=data['latitude'],
            longitude=data['longitude'],
            battery_level=data.get('battery_level', 100),
            is_inside_geofence=gps['is_inside'],
            recorded_at=now,
        )

        if gps['is_inside']:
            local_now = timezone.localtime(now)
            cutoff_time = (local_now - timedelta(minutes=15)).time()
            active_unlogged = Schedule.objects.filter(
                faculty=faculty,
                is_active=True,
                start_time__lte=cutoff_time,
                end_time__gte=local_now.time(),
            ).exclude(attendance_records__attendance_date=local_now.date()).first()

            if active_unlogged:
                LoafingIncident.objects.get_or_create(
                    faculty=faculty,
                    incident_date=local_now.date(),
                    trigger_reason=f'Verified on campus via mobile app but no classroom QR attendance recorded for {active_unlogged.subject.code} within 15 minutes.',
                )

        return Response({
            'success': True,
            'heartbeat_id': hb.id,
            'face_verified': True,
            'is_inside_geofence': gps['is_inside'],
            'distance_meters': gps['distance_meters'],
            'allowed_radius': gps['allowed_radius'],
            'message': f"Facial recognition and GPS verified. {gps['reason']}",
        }, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)


class FacultyDeviceResetAPIView(APIView):
    """Allows administrators on a laptop/PC to clear a faculty member's bound mobile phone."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request, pk=None):
        if not (request.user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(request.user)):
            return Response({'error': 'Only school administrators can reset device bindings.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        faculty_id = pk or request.data.get('faculty_id')
        faculty = FacultyProfile.objects.filter(id=faculty_id).first()
        if not faculty:
            return Response({'error': 'Faculty profile not found.'}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        faculty.bound_device_id = None
        faculty.device_model = None
        faculty.device_bound_at = None
        faculty.save(update_fields=['bound_device_id', 'device_model', 'device_bound_at'])

        return Response({'success': True, 'message': f'Device binding cleared for {faculty.full_name}.'}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

"""
AttendSure - Classroom Cutting-Class & Faculty GPS Loafing Monitor
File: backend/apps/api/views/monitoring.py
"""

from datetime import datetime
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.academics.models import (
    DailyAttendanceSummary,
    Enrollment,
    FacultyHeartbeat,
    GatePass,
    Schedule,
    SubjectAttendanceLog,
)
from .common import (
    format_full_student_name,
    get_faculty_section_ids,
    get_user_role,
    is_leadership_role,
    resolve_faculty_for_user,
)


class LoafingMonitoringAPIView(APIView):
    """
    Detects real-time attendance anomalies from database records:
    1. Students marked PRESENT/LATE in an earlier class today who are missing
       from a current active class without an approved exit pass.
    2. Faculty members whose latest mobile GPS check-in is outside the campus
       boundary during scheduled teaching hours without an approved exit pass.
    """
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        user = request.user
        role = get_user_role(user)
        is_leader = bool(user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(user))

        if not (is_leader or role == 'FACULTY'):
            return Response({'error': 'Unauthorized access.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        today = timezone.localdate()
        now_dt = timezone.now()
        now_time = timezone.localtime(now_dt).time()
        today_start = timezone.make_aware(datetime.combine(today, datetime.min.time()))

        active_student_passes = set(
            GatePass.objects.filter(
                student__isnull=False,
                status='ACTIVE',
                valid_from__lte=now_dt,
                valid_to__gte=now_dt,
            ).values_list('student_id', flat=True)
        )

        active_faculty_passes = set(
            GatePass.objects.filter(
                faculty__isnull=False,
                status='ACTIVE',
                valid_from__lte=now_dt,
                valid_to__gte=now_dt,
            ).values_list('faculty_id', flat=True)
        )

        present_today_ids = set(
            DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.filter(
                attendance_date=today,
                status__in=['PRESENT', 'LATE'],
            ).values_list('student_id', flat=True)
        )

        schedules_qs = Schedule.objects.select_related('section', 'subject', 'faculty').filter(
            is_active=True,
            start_time__lte=now_time,
        )
        if not is_leader:
            own_faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
            if not own_faculty:
                return Response({'anomalies': [], 'total': 0}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
            schedules_qs = schedules_qs.filter(section_id__in=get_faculty_section_ids(own_faculty))

        current_schedules = list(schedules_qs)
        schedule_ids = [s.id for s in current_schedules]
        section_ids = {s.section_id for s in current_schedules if s.section_id}

        verified_pairs = set(
            SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(
                attendance_date=today,
                schedule_id__in=schedule_ids,
                status__in=['PRESENT', 'LATE', 'EXCUSED'],
            ).values_list('student_id', 'schedule_id')
        )
        started_schedule_ids = {sc_id for _, sc_id in verified_pairs}

        enrollments_by_section = {}
        for enr in Enrollment.objects.filter(
            section_id__in=section_ids,
            status='ENROLLED',
            student__is_active=True,
        ).select_related('student'):
            enrollments_by_section.setdefault(enr.section_id, []).append(enr.student)

        anomalies = []
        time_label = timezone.localtime(now_dt).strftime('%I:%M %p')

        # 1. Cross-period student cutting-class detection
        for sched in current_schedules:
            sec = sched.section
            if not sec or sched.id not in started_schedule_ids:
                continue

            for st in enrollments_by_section.get(sec.id, []):
                if (
                    st.id in present_today_ids
                    and (st.id, sched.id) not in verified_pairs
                    and st.id not in active_student_passes
                ):
                    subj_code = sched.subject.code if sched.subject else 'CLASS'
                    anomalies.append({
                        'category': 'STUDENT',
                        'anomaly_type': 'CUTTING_CLASS',
                        'severity': 'HIGH',
                        'person_id': st.id,
                        'lrn': st.lrn,
                        'name': format_full_student_name(st),
                        'section': sec.name,
                        'subject': subj_code,
                        'details': f'Verified in an earlier class today but missing from {subj_code} without an exit pass.',
                        'detected_at': time_label,
                    })

        # 2. Faculty mobile GPS boundary check during active class hours
        if is_leader:
            active_lectures = [
                s for s in current_schedules
                if s.faculty_id and s.end_time and s.end_time >= now_time
            ]
            faculty_ids = {s.faculty_id for s in active_lectures}

            latest_heartbeats = {}
            if faculty_ids:
                for hb in FacultyHeartbeat.objects.filter(
                    faculty_id__in=faculty_ids,
                    recorded_at__gte=today_start,
                ).order_by('faculty_id', '-recorded_at'):
                    latest_heartbeats.setdefault(hb.faculty_id, hb)

            for sched in active_lectures:
                fac = sched.faculty
                if not fac or fac.id in active_faculty_passes:
                    continue
                hb = latest_heartbeats.get(fac.id)
                if hb and not hb.is_inside_geofence:
                    subj_code = sched.subject.code if sched.subject else 'CLASS'
                    anomalies.append({
                        'category': 'FACULTY',
                        'anomaly_type': 'OUTSIDE_GEOFENCE',
                        'severity': 'HIGH',
                        'person_id': fac.id,
                        'employee_id': fac.employee_id,
                        'name': fac.full_name,
                        'section': sched.section.name if sched.section else '',
                        'subject': subj_code,
                        'details': f'Outside campus GPS boundary during scheduled class ({subj_code}) without an exit pass.',
                        'detected_at': timezone.localtime(hb.recorded_at).strftime('%I:%M %p'),
                    })

        return Response({
            'success': True,
            'total': len(anomalies),
            'anomalies': anomalies,
            'results': anomalies,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

"""
AttendSure - Parent Mobile App Portal & Push Notification Views
File: backend/apps/api/views/parent.py
"""

from django.db.models import Q
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.academics.models import (
    AppNotification,
    DailyAttendanceSummary,
    Enrollment,
    GatePass,
    Student,
    SubjectAttendanceLog,
)
from apps.academics.models.people import normalize_ph_phone
from .common import format_deduped_section_label, format_full_student_name, safe_photo_url


class ParentPortalAPIView(APIView):
    """
    Returns real-time classroom attendance, exit passes, and mobile push alerts
    for the children linked to the logged-in parent mobile app account.
    """
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        user = request.user
        today = timezone.localdate()
        profile = getattr(user, 'profile', None)
        phone = normalize_ph_phone(getattr(profile, 'phone_number', '') if profile else '')

        if phone:
            Student.objects.filter(parent_contact=phone, parent_user__isnull=True).update(parent_user=user)

        children_qs = Student.objects.filter(parent_user=user, is_active=True).order_by('first_name')
        children = list(children_qs)
        child_ids = [c.id for c in children]

        enrollments_map = {
            enr.student_id: enr
            for enr in Enrollment.objects.filter(
                student_id__in=child_ids,
                status='ENROLLED',
            ).select_related('section', 'section__grade_level', 'section__adviser')
        }

        daily_map = {
            ds.student_id: ds
            for ds in DailyAttendanceSummary.objects.filter(
                student_id__in=child_ids,
                attendance_date=today,
            )
        }

        subject_logs_by_child = {}
        for log in (
            SubjectAttendanceLog.objects.filter(
                student_id__in=child_ids,
                attendance_date=today,
            )
            .select_related('schedule__subject', 'faculty')
            .order_by('-scanned_at')
        ):
            subject_logs_by_child.setdefault(log.student_id, []).append({
                'id': log.id,
                'subject_code': log.schedule.subject.code if (log.schedule and log.schedule.subject) else 'CLASS',
                'subject_title': log.schedule.subject.title if (log.schedule and log.schedule.subject) else 'Class',
                'faculty_name': log.faculty.full_name if log.faculty else '',
                'status': log.status,
                'scanned_at': timezone.localtime(log.scanned_at).strftime('%I:%M %p') if log.scanned_at else '',
            })

        active_passes_by_child = {}
        for gp in GatePass.objects.filter(
            student_id__in=child_ids,
        ).select_related('issued_by').order_by('-created_at')[:20]:
            active_passes_by_child.setdefault(gp.student_id, []).append({
                'id': gp.id,
                'pass_number': gp.pass_number,
                'pass_type': gp.pass_type,
                'reason': gp.reason,
                'status': gp.status,
                'issued_by': gp.issued_by.full_name if gp.issued_by else 'Adviser',
                'valid_from': timezone.localtime(gp.valid_from).strftime('%I:%M %p') if gp.valid_from else '',
                'valid_to': timezone.localtime(gp.valid_to).strftime('%I:%M %p') if gp.valid_to else '',
            })

        children_data = []
        for child in children:
            enr = enrollments_map.get(child.id)
            sec = enr.section if enr else None
            grade_name = sec.grade_level.name if (sec and sec.grade_level) else ''
            daily = daily_map.get(child.id)

            children_data.append({
                'id': child.id,
                'lrn': child.lrn,
                'first_name': child.first_name,
                'last_name': child.last_name,
                'full_name': format_full_student_name(child),
                'sex': child.sex,
                'photo_url': safe_photo_url(child.photo, request),
                'section': format_deduped_section_label(grade_name, sec.name if sec else 'Unassigned'),
                'adviser_name': sec.adviser.full_name if (sec and sec.adviser) else '',
                'today_status': daily.status if daily else 'UNSCANNED',
                'today_remarks': daily.remarks if daily else 'No classroom scans recorded yet today.',
                'subject_scans_today': subject_logs_by_child.get(child.id, []),
                'exit_passes': active_passes_by_child.get(child.id, []),
            })

        notifications = list(
            AppNotification.objects.filter(
                Q(recipient=user) | Q(student_id__in=child_ids)
            )
            .select_related('student')
            .order_by('-created_at')[:50]
        )
        unread_count = sum(1 for n in notifications if not n.is_read)

        return Response({
            'parent_username': user.username,
            'parent_name': user.get_full_name() or user.username,
            'phone_number': phone,
            'children_count': len(children_data),
            'children': children_data,
            'unread_notifications': unread_count,
            'notifications': [
                {
                    'id': n.id,
                    'student_id': n.student_id,
                    'student_name': n.student.full_name if n.student else '',
                    'title': n.title,
                    'message': n.message,
                    'category': n.category,
                    'is_read': n.is_read,
                    'created_at': timezone.localtime(n.created_at).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %I:%M %p'),
                }
                for n in notifications
            ],
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class MarkNotificationsReadAPIView(APIView):
    """Marks mobile app notifications as read for the authenticated parent or faculty user."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def post(self, request):
        notif_ids = request.data.get('notification_ids') or request.data.get('ids')
        qs = AppNotification.objects.filter(recipient=request.user, is_read=False)
        if isinstance(notif_ids, list) and notif_ids:
            clean_ids = [int(i) for i in notif_ids if str(i).isdigit()]
            qs = qs.filter(id__in=clean_ids)

        updated = qs.update(is_read=True)
        return Response({'success': True, 'marked_read_count': updated}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

"""
AttendSure - DepEd SF1, SF2, SF4, Faculty DTR & Audit Report Views (Batch Optimized)
File: backend/apps/api/views/reports.py

import calendar
from datetime import date
from django.db.models import Count
from django.shortcuts import render
from django.utils import timezone
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

    AuditLog,
    FacultyHeartbeat,
    FacultyMonthlyDTR,
    FacultyProfile,
    Section,
)
from apps.academics.services.sf1_service import get_sf1_data

class IsLeadershipOrAssignedFaculty(permissions.BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        if not request.user or not request.user.is_authenticated:
            return False
        return bool(
            request.user.is_superuser
            or is_leadership_role(request.user)
            or resolve_faculty_for_user(request.user)
        )


class IsLeadershipOnly(permissions.BasePermission):
    def has_permission(self, request, view):
        return bool(
            request.user
            and request.user.is_authenticated
            and (request.user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(request.user))
        )


class DepEdSF1DataAPIView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsLeadershipOrAssignedFaculty]

    def get(self, request, section_id=None):
        raw_section_id = section_id or request.query_params.get('section_id')
        if not raw_section_id or not str(raw_section_id).isdigit():
            return Response({'error': 'Valid section_id is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        sec_id = int(raw_section_id)
        if not (request.user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(request.user)):
            fac = resolve_faculty_for_user(request.user)
            if not fac or sec_id not in get_faculty_section_ids(fac):
                return Response({'error': 'You do not have access to this section.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        try:
            data = get_sf1_data(sec_id, school_year=request.query_params.get('school_year'))
            return Response(data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
        except ValueError as exc:
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)


class DepEdSF2DataAPIView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsLeadershipOrAssignedFaculty]

    def get(self, request, section_id=None):
        raw_section_id = section_id or request.query_params.get('section_id')
        if not raw_section_id or not str(raw_section_id).isdigit():
            return Response({'error': 'Valid section_id is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

        sec_id = int(raw_section_id)
        if not (request.user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(request.user)):
            fac = resolve_faculty_for_user(request.user)
            if not fac or sec_id not in get_faculty_section_ids(fac):
                return Response({'error': 'You do not have access to this section.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        month = request.query_params.get('month', 'October')
        year = request.query_params.get('year', timezone.localdate().year)
        try:
            data = generate_sf2_data(sec_id, month_name=month, year=int(year))
            return Response(data, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
        except ValueError as exc:
            return Response({'error': str(exc)}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

class DTRListAPIView(APIView):
    """Generates faculty monthly DTR records from mobile GPS campus check-ins using batch queries."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsLeadershipOrAssignedFaculty]

    def _build_daily_logs(self, fac, year: int, month: int):
        _, num_days = calendar.monthrange(year, month)
        heartbeats = FacultyHeartbeat.objects.filter(
            faculty=fac,
            recorded_at__year=year,
            recorded_at__month=month,
            is_inside_geofence=True,
        ).order_by('recorded_at')

        pings_by_day = {}
        for hb in heartbeats:
            local_dt = timezone.localtime(hb.recorded_at)
            pings_by_day.setdefault(local_dt.day, []).append(local_dt)

        days = []
        for day_num in range(1, num_days + 1):
            dt_obj = date(year, month, day_num)
            day_pings = pings_by_day.get(day_num, [])
            am_in = day_pings[0].strftime('%I:%M %p') if day_pings else ''
            pm_out = day_pings[-1].strftime('%I:%M %p') if len(day_pings) > 1 else ''
            days.append({
                'day': day_num,
                'date': dt_obj.strftime('%Y-%m-%d'),
                'weekday': dt_obj.strftime('%a'),
                'am_arrival': am_in,
                'am_departure': '12:00 PM' if am_in else '',
                'pm_arrival': '01:00 PM' if pm_out else '',
                'pm_departure': pm_out,
                'status': 'PRESENT' if day_pings else ('WEEKEND' if dt_obj.weekday() >= 5 else 'NO_LOG'),
            })
        return days

    def get(self, request, faculty_id=None):
        user = request.user
        month = int(request.query_params.get('month', timezone.localdate().month))
        year = int(request.query_params.get('year', timezone.localdate().year))
        target_fac_id = faculty_id or request.query_params.get('faculty_id')

        is_leader = bool(user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(user))
        own_fac = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)

        if target_fac_id and str(target_fac_id).isdigit():
            fac_pk = int(target_fac_id)
            if not is_leader and (not own_fac or own_fac.id != fac_pk):
                return Response({'error': 'You can only view your own DTR record.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)
            fac = FacultyProfile.objects.filter(id=fac_pk).first()
            if not fac:
                return Response({'error': 'Faculty profile not found.'}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

            dtr_obj, _ = FacultyMonthlyDTR.objects.get_or_create(faculty=fac, month=month, year=year)
            daily_logs = self._build_daily_logs(fac, year, month)
            days_present = sum(1 for d in daily_logs if d['status'] == 'PRESENT')

            return Response({
                'id': dtr_obj.id,
                'faculty_id': fac.id,
                'employee_id': fac.employee_id,
                'faculty_name': fac.full_name,
                'position': fac.position,
                'department': fac.department,
                'month': month,
                'year': year,
                'days_present': days_present,
                'is_submitted': dtr_obj.is_submitted,
                'is_dept_head_approved': dtr_obj.is_dept_head_approved,
                'regular_hours': dtr_obj.regular_hours,
                'saturday_hours': dtr_obj.saturday_hours,
                'daily_logs': daily_logs,
                'school': get_school_metadata(request),
            }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

        if is_leader:
            faculty_list = list(FacultyProfile.objects.filter(is_active=True).order_by('last_name', 'first_name'))
        else:
            faculty_list = [own_fac] if own_fac else []

        fac_ids = [f.id for f in faculty_list]
        existing_dtrs = {
            d.faculty_id: d
            for d in FacultyMonthlyDTR.objects.filter(faculty_id__in=fac_ids, month=month, year=year)
        }
        missing_dtrs = [
            FacultyMonthlyDTR(faculty_id=f.id, month=month, year=year)
            for f in faculty_list if f.id not in existing_dtrs
        ]
        if missing_dtrs:
            FacultyMonthlyDTR.objects.bulk_create(missing_dtrs, ignore_conflicts=True)
            existing_dtrs = {
                d.faculty_id: d
                for d in FacultyMonthlyDTR.objects.filter(faculty_id__in=fac_ids, month=month, year=year)
            }

        present_counts = dict(
            FacultyHeartbeat.objects.filter(
                faculty_id__in=fac_ids,
                recorded_at__year=year,
                recorded_at__month=month,
                is_inside_geofence=True,
            )
            .values('faculty_id')
            .annotate(days=Count('recorded_at__date', distinct=True))
            .values_list('faculty_id', 'days')
        )

        dtr_records = []
        for fac in faculty_list:
            dtr_obj = existing_dtrs.get(fac.id)
            dtr_records.append({
                'id': dtr_obj.id if dtr_obj else fac.id,
                'faculty_id': fac.id,
                'employee_id': fac.employee_id,
                'faculty_name': fac.full_name,
                'position': fac.position,
                'department': fac.department,
                'month': month,
                'year': year,
                'days_present': present_counts.get(fac.id, 0),
                'is_submitted': bool(dtr_obj and dtr_obj.is_submitted),
                'is_dept_head_approved': bool(dtr_obj and dtr_obj.is_dept_head_approved),
            })

        return Response({'month': month, 'year': year, 'records': dtr_records}, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class UnifiedReportExportAPIView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsLeadershipOrAssignedFaculty]

    def get(self, request):
        form_type = str(request.query_params.get('form', 'SF2')).upper()
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section_id')
        month = request.query_params.get('month', 'October')
        year = int(request.query_params.get('year', timezone.localdate().year))

        if form_type == 'SF1' and section_id and str(section_id).isdigit():
            return Response(get_sf1_data(int(section_id)), status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
        if form_type == 'SF4':
            return Response(generate_sf4_data(month_name=month, year=year), status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
        if section_id and str(section_id).isdigit():
            return Response(generate_sf2_data(int(section_id), month_name=month, year=year), status=status.HTTP_200_OK)
        return Response({'error': 'Valid section_id is required for SF1/SF2.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)


class UnifiedReportPrintAPIView(APIView):
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsLeadershipOrAssignedFaculty]

    def get(self, request):
        section_id = request.query_params.get('section_id')
        if not section_id or not str(section_id).isdigit():
            return Response({'error': 'Valid section_id is required.'}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
        section = Section.objects.select_related('grade_level', 'academic_year', 'adviser').filter(id=int(section_id)).first()
        if not section:
            return Response({'error': 'Section not found.'}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)
        return render(request, 'reports/sf1_template.html', {
            'school': get_school_metadata(request),
            'section': section,
        })

    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated, IsLeadershipOnly]

                    'id': item.id,
                    'table_name': item.table_name,
                    'record_id': item.record_id,
                    'user': item.performed_by.username if item.performed_by else 'System',
                    'created_at': timezone.localtime(item.created_at).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %I:%M %p'),
                }
                for item in logs
            ]
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

AttendSure - Section & Class List Views

from django.db.models import Count, Q
from rest_framework import permissions, status
from rest_framework.response import Response
from rest_framework.views import APIView

from apps.academics.models import Enrollment, Schedule, Section
from .common import (
    format_deduped_section_label,
    format_full_student_name,
    get_faculty_section_ids,
    is_leadership_role,
    resolve_faculty_for_user,
    safe_photo_url,
)


class SectionListAPIView(APIView):
    """Returns active sections with enrolled student counts in a single batch query."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request):
        user = request.user
        qs = (
            Section.objects.filter(is_active=True)
            .select_related('grade_level', 'academic_year', 'adviser', 'room')
            .annotate(
                enrolled_count=Count(
                    'enrollments',
                    filter=Q(enrollments__status='ENROLLED', enrollments__student__is_active=True),
                    distinct=True,
                )
            )
            .order_by('grade_level__level_order', 'name')
        )

        if not (user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(user)):
            faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
            if faculty:
                qs = qs.filter(id__in=get_faculty_section_ids(faculty))
            else:
                qs = qs.none()

        results = []
        for sec in qs:
            grade_name = sec.grade_level.name if sec.grade_level else ''
            results.append({
                'id': sec.id,
                'name': sec.name,
                'grade_level': grade_name,
                'grade_level_id': sec.grade_level_id,
                'academic_year': sec.academic_year.code if sec.academic_year else '',
                'adviser_id': sec.adviser_id,
                'adviser_name': sec.adviser.full_name if sec.adviser else 'Unassigned',
                'room_name': sec.room.name if sec.room else sec.room_number,
                'capacity': sec.capacity,
                'enrolled_count': sec.enrolled_count,
                'display_label': format_deduped_section_label(grade_name, sec.name),
            })

        return Response(results, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)


class SectionDetailAPIView(APIView):
    """Returns a section's details, uniform subject schedules, and enrolled class list."""
    permission_classes = [permissions.IsAuthenticated]

    def get(self, request, pk: int):
        user = request.user
        sec = (
            Section.objects.select_related('grade_level', 'academic_year', 'adviser', 'room')
            .filter(id=pk)
            .first()
        )
        if not sec:
            return Response({'error': 'Section not found.'}, status=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND)

        if not (user.is_superuser or is_leadership_role(user)):
            faculty = resolve_faculty_for_user(user)
            if not faculty or sec.id not in get_faculty_section_ids(faculty):
                return Response({'error': 'Access denied to this section.'}, status=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN)

        enrollments = (
            Enrollment.objects.filter(section=sec, status='ENROLLED', student__is_active=True)
            .select_related('student')
            .order_by('student__last_name', 'student__first_name')
        )
        class_list = [
            {
                'id': enr.student.id,
                'lrn': enr.student.lrn,
                'full_name': format_full_student_name(enr.student),
                'sex': enr.student.sex,
                'parent_contact': enr.student.parent_contact,
                'qr_token': enr.student.qr_token,
                'photo_url': safe_photo_url(enr.student.photo, request),
            }
            for enr in enrollments if enr.student
        ]

        schedules = (
            Schedule.objects.filter(section=sec, is_active=True)
            .select_related('subject', 'faculty', 'room')
            .order_by('start_time')
        )
        schedule_items = [
            {
                'id': s.id,
                'subject_id': s.subject_id,
                'subject_code': s.subject.code if s.subject else '',
                'subject_title': s.subject.title if s.subject else '',
                'faculty_id': s.faculty_id,
                'faculty_name': s.faculty.full_name if s.faculty else 'Unassigned',
                'room_name': s.room.name if s.room else s.room_number,
                'start_time': str(s.start_time)[:5] if s.start_time else '',
                'end_time': str(s.end_time)[:5] if s.end_time else '',
            }
            for s in schedules
        ]

        grade_name = sec.grade_level.name if sec.grade_level else ''
        return Response({
            'id': sec.id,
            'name': sec.name,
            'grade_level': grade_name,
            'academic_year': sec.academic_year.code if sec.academic_year else '',
            'adviser_id': sec.adviser_id,
            'adviser_name': sec.adviser.full_name if sec.adviser else 'Unassigned',
            'room_name': sec.room.name if sec.room else sec.room_number,
            'capacity': sec.capacity,
            'display_label': format_deduped_section_label(grade_name, sec.name),
            'total_students': len(class_list),
            'class_list': class_list,
            'students': class_list,
            'schedules': schedule_items,
        }, status=status.HTTP_200_OK)

class PathParamReportMixin:
    """
    Allows report views to accept IDs either in the URL path (/api/reports/sf1/24/)
    or in query parameters (?section_id=24 or ?faculty_id=19).
    """
    param_keys = ('id', 'pk', 'section_id', 'section', 'faculty_id', 'faculty')

    def dispatch(self, request, *args, **kwargs):
        pk_val = (
            kwargs.get('pk')
            or kwargs.get('section_id')
            or kwargs.get('faculty_id')
        )
        if pk_val is not None:
            query_copy = request.GET.copy()
            for key in self.param_keys:
                query_copy.setdefault(key, str(pk_val))
            request.GET = query_copy
            request.META['QUERY_STRING'] = query_copy.urlencode()

            handler = getattr(self, request.method.lower(), None)
            if handler:
                sig = inspect.signature(handler)
                accepts_var_kw = any(
                    p.kind == inspect.Parameter.VAR_KEYWORD
                    for p in sig.parameters.values()
                )
                if not accepts_var_kw:
                    for k in list(kwargs.keys()):
                        if k not in sig.parameters:
                            kwargs.pop(k, None)

        return super().dispatch(request, *args, **kwargs)
